And the order's own two clauses were in tension: its first outcome presupposes a cross term while its third called a failure to sum the finding. `cc66` took the coherent one and built the gate the order should have asked for. ⛔ *And the cross term --- this seat's lead hypothesis, argued from $j_\ell$ and $j_\ell'$ being a quarter period apart --- is **excluded at about a part in a thousand**.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE PER-TERM LADDER IS WHAT REPLACES IT, WHICH IS A BIGGER OBJECT THAN THE HYPOTHESIS IT KILLED.*** *Every term's source ratio is at or below $0.992$ and **every term's projected ratio is higher** --- monopole $+0.042$, Doppler $+0.126$, ISW $+0.017$, polarisation $+0.067$, the whole source $+0.053$. ⇒ ***So the excess is not localised in the source at all, at any grain: the projection raises this arm's contrast on nearly everything it projects.*** ⌗ *And with the intercept straddling one while the slope never does, the projection does nothing different at the longest acoustic wavelengths and more of it at each shorter one --- which excludes an overall normalisation difference by the same fit that establishes the rise. `P15` carries that as a clause of its own, because it is what rules things out.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND THE REFRAMING THE LADDER FORCES, WHICH IS WHERE THE ORDER GOES NEXT.*** *Write the reported spectrum as $\Delta_\ell(k)=\int S\,j_\ell(k\chi(\eta))\,\dd\eta$. **Every term decomposed so far lives in $S$, and what raises this arm's contrast is common to nearly every $S$ there is.** ⇒ *So there are exactly two things left in that integral and neither is a source term: the kernel, which is the same function on both arms, and $\chi(\eta)$ together with the visibility's weight in $\eta$ --- **the effective width the projection averages over**, which is term-independent by construction and grows with wavenumber because at fixed width there are more acoustic periods to average away at each shorter one, going to no difference at all as the window falls below one period. That last clause is the measured intercept.* ⚠ ***And the candidate is named so it can be killed rather than left implicit: this construction carries TWO RATES***, the plasma's accumulated lengths on the leaf and comoving separations read across leaves on the stacking, *so the source is accumulated on one clock while the kernel's argument is a distance read on the other* --- **a candidate and not a claim, with nothing yet checked about what the instrument integrates against what.* &#9670; ⛐ ***THE ORDER ROUTED, AND IT TAKES THE SOURCE OUT OF THE QUESTION.*** *Ⓐ feed both arms' projection machinery the **same analytic oscillating source** --- no transfer, no terms, no weights --- and measure the retained fraction against $q$: reproducing the $1.054$ and the $+0.0139$ shows the effect is the projection's geometry and **the source is irrelevant to it**, while failing to reproduce them puts it in how $S$ is distributed across the visibility and kills the paragraph above. Ⓑ then **swap the two geometric factors one at a time** --- this arm's source with the control's visibility, and this arm's source through the control's $\chi(\eta)$ --- with the standing instruction that **an ill-posed swap is to be reported as ill-posed rather than forced**, that being the shape of `r4558`'s unwired knob.* &#9670; ⛭⚭⚭ ***AND r6925 (66, on cc66.42) ENDS THE HUNT: THE MECHANISM IS THE CORPUS'S OWN RATE ASSIGNMENT.*** *A pure $g(\eta)\cos(kr_s(\eta))$ --- **no transfer, no terms, no weights**, carrying $k^{(1-n_s)/2}$ so the smooth part is exactly $\dd k/k$ on both arms --- projected through each arm's own kernel, visibility, wavenumber grid and multipole grid gives $1.066$ at $+0.0226$ per unit $q$, against the real source's $1.054$ and $+0.0139$. ⇒ ***The geometry accounts for the whole effect and OVER-delivers***, phase-independently, with a standing oscillation already carrying most of it.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THIS SEAT'S NAMED LOCUS WAS WRONG WHILE ITS OBJECT WAS RIGHT.*** *$x_0=\eta_0-\mathrm{EE}$ on both arms, so $\chi(\eta)=\eta_0-\eta$ and $\dd\chi/\dd\eta\equiv1$ on each, with nothing touching $x_0$ on any path --- **there is nothing there to exchange**. ⇒ ***The two clocks sit between $r_s$ and $\eta$***: conformal time, and so $x_0$, is built from the stacking rate while the acoustic phase accumulates on the leaf's, and $\dd\eta_{\rm leaf}/\dd\eta_{\rm stack}$ is $1.000000$ everywhere on the control by the rate identity and runs $0.789$ to $0.913$ across $\pm3$ FWHM on the arm.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE JOINT OBJECT IS THE SIGNATURE, WITH ITS THREE PROPERTIES NOW CAUSED RATHER THAN MEASURED.*** *Across the visibility FWHM the accumulated **sound horizon** agrees to $0.08$ per cent ($17.3074$ against $17.2941$~Mpc) while the **comoving distance** differs by $14.6$ ($38.042$ against $43.591$). So $\dd r_s/\dd\chi$ --- ***the sound speed the kernel sees*** --- is $0.4550$ on the control against $0.3967$ here, $12.8$ per cent lower: **term-independent by construction, growing with wavenumber, and vanishing for a window under one acoustic period**, which are exactly `cc66.41`'s three properties.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND NEITHER SWAP CLOSES IT ALONE, WHICH WAS THE ORDER'S THIRD OUTCOME.*** *The clock swap is **ill posed as an isolation and the statistic says so rather than returning a null**: it moves the comb and the regression alternates in sign band by band, $+0.37$ to $-0.57$ --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing on the shape it was built for, ***and itself evidence that the leaf assignment is what the arm's reported peak positions need***. The width swap is well posed and **overshoots by eight**, the slope going $+0.0226$ to $+0.0931$ while the mean barely moves.*\rcpt{P15_a_source_with_no_physics_reproduces_the_contrast_and_the_two_clocks_part_company_at_the_visibility} &#9670; ⛭ ***SO THE ROW CHANGES ITS QUESTION, WHICH IS THE POINT.*** *The five per cent is no longer unexplained: **it is the instrument doing what the corpus's two-rate assignment tells it to.** ⇒ ***What is open is whether that assignment is right, not whether the transfer implements it*** --- and `P15` places the residual there in terms. ⚠ *And the rule has a gap the order now aims at: it covers separations read across leaves and scales the plasma accumulates, and **the visibility is neither** --- $\tau$ is accumulated by the plasma but differentiated per unit $\eta$, which is the stacking rate's, so nothing in the rule says which clock $g$ is a density in.* &#9670; ⛭⚭ ***AND r6929 (66, on cc66.43) ANSWERS THE RULE'S GAP: THE VISIBILITY IS NOT WHERE THE FREEDOM IS.*** *The audit found **the gap already open in the code**: every site where $\tau$ or $g$ touches a rate is on the STACKING clock --- the recombination history against $H_{\rm phys}$, the conformal grid, `ETA_LS` read off it --- while $1/k_D^{2}$ twenty lines below **is** weighted under `LEAFSCALES`. ⇒ ***So the diffusion length and the optical depth are both scales the plasma accumulates, sitting on opposite clocks, with nothing stating the choice*** --- which is what makes the other assignment admissible rather than invented, applying to $\tau$ exactly the weighting $1/k_D^{2}$ already applies to itself.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND ON THE GEOMETRY THE TWO AGREE, STRUCTURALLY RATHER THAN LUCKILY.*** *$\dd r_s/\dd\chi$ goes $0.396733$ to $0.396957$, so $12.80$ per cent becomes $12.75$ --- a move of $0.06$ --- and the control does not move at all. **Because it is a RATIO of two accumulations across the SAME window, so re-weighting the measure re-weights both.** ⇒ *The visibility is not where the freedom is, and the $12.8$ per cent is forced by the rule as stated.* &#9670; ⚠ ***BUT THE CONTRAST AND THE COMB DISAGREE, AND NEITHER IS PICKED.*** *Read alone the other assignment moves the injection toward the real source, $1.0850\to1.0695$ and slope $+0.0242\to+0.0133$ against the real $1.0694$ and $+0.0117$ --- **but moves the arm's first peak $220\to228$ and $\ell_1/\ell_A$ $0.7290\to0.7555$, AWAY from the sky's $0.7312$**, with $P_1/P_2$ $2.142\to2.017$ and the control's comb bit-identical. And the contrast improvement is partly that same move read by the statistic: band by band it is non-monotonic scatter with a fitted residual **nine times** the current assignment's, because moving `ETA_LS` moves $r_s(\mathrm{ETA\_LS})$ and so moves the comb --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing a third time. ⇒ ***The comb is the only one of the three readings with an external referent, and it supports the assignment the instrument has.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE CHAT SEAT'S CANCELLATION HYPOTHESIS IS HALF RIGHT, WHICH IS THE USEFUL SPLIT.*** *The injection times the measured source deficit --- $0.9922$, **flat in $q$** at $-0.00106$ --- gives $1.0766$ at $+0.02296$ against the real $1.0694$ at $+0.01167$: **$54$ per cent of the level gap and $10$ per cent of the slope gap.** So the four trough-filling channels account for about half the level and essentially none of the slope, and ***the sector does NOT close into one account***.*\rcpt{P15_the_visibilitys_clock_is_the_one_the_kernel_reads_and_the_comb_votes_against_the_alternative} &#9670; ⚠ ***AND ONE NEAR-MISS RECORDED AGAINST THE CODE SEAT BY ITSELF, WHICH THE ROW KEEPS BECAUSE IT IS A CLASS AND NOT AN INCIDENT.*** *A launcher's positional-argument bug --- `shift 4` then `$5 $6 $7 $8` --- ran thirty-six slices as the unset switch: **they completed, reported nothing wrong, and reproduced the banked spectra.** ⇒ *That is the shape that gets banked as an answer, and the same family as `r4558`'s unwired knob and `cc66.36`'s knob shadow --- a switch not reaching the physics while everything downstream looks healthy. The fix is a smoke test greping the log for the marker the switch must print, and the order asks for it standing rather than per-launcher.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, NARROWED AGAIN:** *what makes the projection treat the two arms differently on **every** term at once and increasingly with wavenumber --- not a term, not a pair, not their interference, and not the distance, the grid, the lensing or the visibility width acting before the kernel. The first thing to rule out is still whether a projection **can** differ between two arms whose acoustic angles agree to a part in a thousand.* &#9670; ⛑⛑⛑ ***THE SOURCE IS IRRELEVANT TO IT AND THE TWO CLOCKS PART COMPANY AT THE VISIBILITY --- r6919+cc66.42.*** *A source with **no physics in it** --- a pure $g(\eta)\cos(k r_s(\eta))$, no transfer, no terms, no weights --- projected through each arm's own kernel, visibility, $k$ grid and multipole grid gives an arm-to-control retained ratio of $\mathbf{1.066}$ at a slope of $\mathbf{+0.0226}$ per unit $q$, against the real source's $1.054$ and $+0.0139$.* ⇒ ***The projection's geometry accounts for the whole of the effect and over-delivers.*** ⌗ *The injection's phase does not matter, and a **standing** oscillation already carries most of it ($1.059/+0.0119$).* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THIS SEAT'S NAMED CANDIDATE IS IN THE INSTRUMENT ONE LAYER OVER FROM WHERE IT WAS PUT.*** *The order proposed that $\chi(\eta)$ is a distance read on the other clock. **`x0 = eta_0 - EE` on both arms, so $\chi(\eta)=\eta_0-\eta$ and $\mathrm d\chi/\mathrm d\eta\equiv1$ identically on each, with no rate touching `x0` on any path --- there is nothing there to exchange.*** ⇒ ***The two clocks sit between $r_s$ and $\eta$***: *conformal time, and so `x0`, is `Hphys`'s --- the **stacking** rate --- while the acoustic phase accumulates on the **leaf** rate, and $\mathrm d\eta_{\rm leaf}/\mathrm d\eta_{\rm stack}$ is $1.000000$ everywhere on the control by the rate identity against $\mathbf{0.789}$--$\mathbf{0.913}$ across the arm's visibility.* &#9670; ⛑⛑⛑ ***AND THAT IS WHERE THEY PART COMPANY, AS ONE NUMBER.*** *Across the visibility FWHM the accumulated **sound horizon** agrees to $\mathbf{0.08}$ per cent ($17.3074$ against $17.2941$~Mpc) while the **comoving distance** differs by $\mathbf{14.6}$ ($38.042$ against $43.591$) --- the leaf clock makes $r_s$ accumulate more slowly per unit $\eta$, so a fifteen per cent wider window covers the **same** acoustic phase and the kernel, which reads $\chi$, sees the wider window.* ⇒ ***The joint object is $\mathrm d r_s/\mathrm d\chi$ across the visibility --- the sound speed the kernel sees --- $0.4550$ on the control against $0.3967$ on the arm, $12.8$ per cent lower***; *term-independent, growing with wavenumber, and vanishing for a window under one acoustic period, which are the three properties `cc66.41` measured.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND NEITHER SWAP CLOSES IT ALONE, the third outcome this seat named.*** *The **clock** swap is **ill posed** as an isolation and the statistic says so rather than returning a null: forcing both arms onto the stacking clock **moves the comb** and the regression alternates in sign band by band, $+0.37$ to $-0.57$ --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing on exactly the shape it was built for, and itself evidence that the leaf assignment is what the arm's reported peak positions need. The **width** swap **is** well posed and **overshoots by eight**: the slope goes $+0.0226$ to $\mathbf{+0.0931}$ while the mean barely moves.*\rcpt{P15_a_source_with_no_physics_reproduces_the_contrast_and_the_two_clocks_part_company_at_the_visibility} &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, NARROWED TO ONE RATIO AND NO LONGER A PROJECTION QUESTION:** *why this construction assigns the scales the plasma accumulates and the distances the kernel reads to **different rates**, which is `P15` \S`sec:tensions`' own question.* ⛑ ***The five per cent is no longer unexplained: it is the instrument doing what the corpus's two-rate assignment tells it to, and what is open is whether that assignment is right.*****&#9670; ⛑⛑ ***AND THE RULE'S GAP IS ALREADY OPEN IN THE CODE --- r6925+cc66.43.*** *The rule does not say which clock $g=\tau'e^{-\tau}$ is a density in, and **the instrument already answers that twice and differently**: every site where $\tau$ or $g$ touches a rate is on the **stacking** clock --- the recombination history against `Hphys`, $\tau'$ on `eg`, $\tau$ over the $\eta$ grid, $\eta_{\rm LS}$ and its FWHM off that grid --- while $1/k_D^2$ **is** Jac-weighted under `LEAFSCALES`.* ⇒ ***So the diffusion length takes the leaf clock and the optical depth the stacking clock: two objects on the same side of the rule, on opposite clocks, with nothing stating the choice.*** &#9670; ⛑⛑ ***ON THE GEOMETRY THE TWO ASSIGNMENTS AGREE:*** *$\mathrm dr_s/\mathrm d\chi$ goes $0.396733\to0.396957$ on the arm, so $12.80$ per cent lower becomes $12.75$ --- **a move of $0.06$ per cent** --- and the control does not move at all. And structurally rather than luckily: it is a **ratio** of two accumulations across the **same** window, so re-weighting the measure re-weights both.* ⇒ ***The visibility is not where the freedom is, and the $12.8$ per cent is forced by the rule as stated.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***BUT THE CONTRAST AND THE COMB DISAGREE, AND NEITHER IS PICKED.*** *Read alone the other assignment moves the injection's retained ratio $1.0850\to1.0695$ and its slope $+0.0242\to+0.0133$, **toward** the real source's $1.0694$ and $+0.0117$. **But the arm's first peak moves $220\to228$ and $\ell_1/\ell_A$ $0.7290\to0.7555$, away from the sky's $0.7312$**, with $P_1/P_2$ $2.142\to2.017$ and the control's comb bit-identical.* ⚠ *And the contrast improvement is partly that same move read by the statistic: band by band the `VISLEAF=1` ratio is **non-monotonic scatter** with a fitted residual **nine times** the current assignment's --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing a third time on the shape it was built for.* ⇒ ***The geometry says forced, the contrast says improvable, the comb says no; the comb is the only one of the three with an external referent, and it supports the assignment the instrument already has.*** &#9670; ⛑ ***AND THE SOURCE'S CANCELLATION IS HALF THE LEVEL AND NONE OF THE SLOPE:*** *the injection times `cc66.40`'s measured source deficit --- $0.9922$, **flat** in $q$ at $-0.00106$ --- gives $1.0766$ at $+0.02296$ against the real $1.0694$ at $+0.01167$, so **$54$ per cent of the level gap and $10$ per cent of the slope gap**. The four trough-filling channels account for about half the level and essentially none of the slope: **the sector does not close into one account**, and what flattens the slope is the real source's own $\eta$-dependence across the visibility.*\rcpt{P15_the_visibilitys_clock_is_the_one_the_kernel_reads_and_the_comb_votes_against_the_alternative} &#9670; ⛑ ***AND AT `r6929+cc66.44` THE ARBITER'S OWN RESOLUTION IS MEASURED, AND IT DECIDES LESS THAN IT LOOKED:*** *the assignment scanned CONTINUOUSLY --- `VISLEAF` a fraction, $f=0$ the stacking clock and $f=1$ the leaf's --- gives $\ell_1 = 221.93 + 4.21f$ to three hundredths of a multipole, so the family's entire span is $0.01395$ in $\ell_1/\ell_A$: $4.2$ of the sky's one-multipole locating widths and $2.1$ of its two-multipole ones.* ⇒ ***The comb pins $f$ to $\pm0.24$ and no further.*** *The order's first branch is half right: the contrast's level is shallow ($1.75\sigma$) but its $q$-slope is the comb's equal ($3.80\sigma$) --- **what separates them is that the contrast's error grows five-fold across the family while the comb's locating width does not move**.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND THE SKY'S OWN VALUE LIES OUTSIDE THE ADMISSIBLE FAMILY:*** *$0.7312$ sits at $f = -0.304$ on the family's line, on the far side of the stacking clock, so **no interior fraction fits better than the endpoint in use** (no fitted clock to declare, and the no-early-parameter claim is not asked to answer for one) and ***the residual first-peak disagreement cannot be absorbed by the clock assignment at all, the direction it would need being inadmissible***. $P_1/P_2$ agrees independently, $2.141\to2.017$ against the sky's $2.217$.* ⚑ *And the guard separates: the comb's motion is $31\%$ the peak relocating through $r_s(\mathrm{ETA\_LS})$, $9\%$ the visibility's re-weighting of the kernel, and $60\%$ the plasma's own acoustic phase.* ⚠ *Two of `cc66.43`'s numbers were GRID-LIMITED and are corrected here: $\ell_1$ $220\to228$ is $221.95\to226.16$ and $\ell_1/\ell_A$ $0.7290\to0.7555$ is $0.73543\to0.74938$ --- the arm sits $1.28$ multipoles **above** the sky at $f=0$, not below --- while the FWHM's $43.591\to43.952$ is exactly one step of the $\eta$ grid and is not a resolved motion.*\rcpt{P15_the_combs_resolution_is_four_multipoles_and_the_skys_own_value_lies_outside_the_family} &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND AT `r6941+cc66.45` THE INSTRUMENT IS CLEARED AND THE FOURTH PEAK'S RESIDUAL TURNS OUT TO BE THE CONTROL'S:*** *the reported configuration re-run at `LSTEP=1` --- $1900$ multipoles against $238$, both arms --- has the reported grid's locator recovering the fine-grid peak to $0.0007/0.0126/0.0283/0.0176$ of a multipole on the control and $0.0035/0.0089/0.0167/0.0229$ on the arm, so **two hundredths at $\ell_4$ against the order's bar of ten**, with the extremum search insensitive to its own width.* ⛔ *What IS imprecise is the parabola's **window**, a bias growing with peak index to $5.8$ (arm) and $6.5$ (control) multipoles at $\ell_4$ --- comparable to the residual there, and displacing the sky and both models alike, which is what matched-procedure differencing is for.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND THE FOUR-PEAK PATTERN IS TWO ARTEFACTS.*** *The quartet $222/538/818/1134$ is `sec:refit-bound`'s line-of-sight path; on the hierarchy path every bank here is on, the raw reading is $220/540/812/1132$ and **at $\ell_3$ the two bracketing bins differ by four parts in ten thousand**, so the grid-point reading there is a coin flip. Sub-bin the arm is $221.96/536.11/815.40/1130.53$: the residual is $+1.56/-1.60/-1.90/+6.63$, **peaks two and three were never on**, and peak four is $6.6$ out rather than $10.1$. And the **control** carries four fifths of it, $+5.32$ of $+6.63$, so what is the construction's is $+1.61/-0.19/+1.07/+1.31$ ---* ***one near-constant multipole at all four peaks, a constant $\Delta\ell$ fitting half again better than the ruler's constant $\Delta\ell/\ell$, and every one of the four inside the sky's own locating spread `PO-47` measured.*** ⇒ **So the pattern needs ONE systematic and not two, and the sky cannot tell it from zero.** ⚑ *The three-way separation at every peak index: relocation $31\to94/83/89$ per cent, the visibility's weighting $9\to26/23/24$, the plasma $60\to-21/-6/-13$ --- **$\ell_1$ is the outlier and nothing else is** --- and the shares are neither flat nor reaching the residual, which is a statement about the guard's coverage.*\rcpt{P15_the_locator_is_good_to_three_hundredths_and_the_fourth_peaks_residual_is_the_controls_too} &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND AT `r6955+cc66.46` THE EXCESS IS LOCATED AS SYMMETRIC AND THE TROUGHS TURN OUT TO BE WHERE THE SKY MEASURES IT BEST:*** *the band variance of the envelope-normalised oscillation split at its own zero gives $1.0668$ on the peak side against $1.0561$ on the trough side, and at the literal extrema the arm exceeds the control by $5.7$ per cent at the maxima against $5.3$ at the minima --* **an AMPLITUDE excess, so $\Delta$'s trough dominance is about the whitened residual and does not transfer to the contrast's own decomposition.** *Across the `VISLEAF` family the depths move $-2.58$ per cent against the heights' $-0.77$, three times more --- but $\theta_D/\theta_*$ moves $-4.08$, because $r_D$ is itself `Jac`-weighted under `LEAFSCALES`, so* ***the paper's discriminant of principle is the most sensitive of the three and the depths are NOT a new handle on the clock.*** &#9670; ⛭ ***What makes the troughs the place to look is the SKY:*** *with `COV_TT` propagated through the identical anchored locator its spread is $0.00588$ on the depth against $0.01135$ on the height, while the arm-minus-control excess is the same size in both ($+0.0106$, $+0.0103$), so it reads* ***$+1.81\sigma$ in the depths against $+0.91$ in the heights --- and the control lands on the sky's depths at $-0.05\sigma$ while the arm sits $+1.76$ above, the first statistic in this sector whose residual is NOT shared with the control.*** ⚑ *The anchored depth carries no window bias at all (identical over $W=20\ldots70$: it reads a value, not a location), while a free search under noise reproduces `PO-47`'s trap at six times the spread.*\rcpt{P15_the_contrast_excess_is_symmetric_about_the_envelope_and_the_troughs_are_where_the_sky_measures_it_best} &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND r6955 (66, gating `cc66.45`) LANDS THE THREE PAPER-SIDE CONSEQUENCES, WHICH WERE ROUTED RATHER THAN APPLIED.*** *`P15` `sec:refit-bound` no longer says the first three peaks land "within a grid step" --- **that was a statement about the grid and not a measurement** --- and says instead that they agree inside the spread the locating itself carries. ⌗ *And the window bias is stated where the one-procedure requirement is asserted, because it is the quantitative reason for it: a fraction of a multipole at the first peak against some six at the fourth, moving the sky and both models alike, so **it cancels from a difference taken through one procedure and from nothing else**.** ⇒ *The offsets' shape is now the differenced quartet $+1.61/-0.19/+1.07/+1.31$ --- one near-constant multipole, stated as a constant displacement against a fractional one that would have to grow from half a multipole to two and a half --- replacing "though not a clean constant", which the sub-bin location has superseded.* ⌗ *And the independent-reproduction passage carries the sub-bin quartet $221.96/536.11/815.40/1130.53$ in place of the raw grid points, so the $\ell_3$ entry that sat on a four-parts-in-ten-thousand height difference between two bins is gone.* &#9670; ⌗ ***ONE JUDGEMENT MADE RATHER THAN ROUTED BACK, AND IT IS ABOUT WHICH PATH OWNS WHICH NUMBER.*** *`cc66.45`'s sub-bin measurement is on the **hierarchy** path; `sec:refit-bound`'s quartet is the **line-of-sight** path's. So the sub-bin figures went into the independent-reproduction passage where that path is the subject, and the line-of-sight quartet is untouched --- **only its grid-step framing moved.** *Importing one path's located apexes into the other path's sentence would have been the same error the quartet's provenance mix-up was.** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND `cc66.46` (gated r6959) ANSWERS ALL THREE PARTS, REFUTES THE ORDER'S ARGUMENT, AND CONFIRMS ITS CONCLUSION BY A DIFFERENT ROUTE --- AND IT COST NO SOLVER TIME, being analysis on banks `cc66.44` and `cc66.45` had already built.*** &#9670; ⛭ ***THE EXCESS IS SYMMETRIC ABOUT THE ENVELOPE: AN AMPLITUDE EXCESS CARRIED EQUALLY BY HEIGHTS AND DEPTHS.*** *Splitting the band variance of the envelope-normalised oscillation at its own zero --- a decomposition, the parts adding to the whole to machine precision --- gives $1.0668$ on the peak side against $1.0561$ on the trough side, and at the literal extrema the arm exceeds the control by $5.7$ per cent at the maxima against $5.3$ at the minima.* ⇒ ***So the order's caveat was right and its premise was not: $\Delta$'s trough dominance is a statement about the WHITENED RESIDUAL and does not transfer to the contrast's own decomposition.*** ⌗ *And the order's stop condition does not fire either --- the excess is not in the peaks, so the row is not pushed back onto the driving.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE ORDER'S CLOCK ARGUMENT IS WRONG, WITH THE STEP IT MISSED NAMED.*** *Across the `VISLEAF` family the mean trough depth moves $-2.58$ per cent against the mean peak height's $-0.77$, so **the depths ARE $3.4\times$ the more clock-responsive and that half of the reading holds.** But $\theta_D/\theta_*$ moves $-4.08$ per cent --- **more than either**.* ⇒ *** The step missed is that $r_D$ is itself Jacobian-weighted under `LEAFSCALES`, so the optical-depth re-weighting moves the diffusion length DIRECTLY: the ratio is not protected by riding one clock, because the clock re-weighting moves its numerator. *** ⇒⇒ **So the paper's discriminant of principle is the MOST sensitive of the three and not the insensitive one, and by the order's own rule --- both move together --- the depths are not a new handle on the clock.** ⌗ *Endpoints reproduce on the unit-step grid, so the response is the spectra's and not the grid's.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND YET THE TROUGHS ARE WHERE IT BECOMES OBSERVABLE, FOR A REASON THAT IS THE SKY'S AND NOT THE MODEL'S: THE SKY MEASURES DEPTHS TWICE AS WELL.*** *Same data, same likelihood binning, same anchored locator, the temperature covariance propagated by Monte Carlo over two seeds and $600$ realisations each, stable to $5\times10^{-4}$. **The arm exceeds the control by the same amount in each --- $+0.01064$ in depth against $+0.01029$ in height --- while the sky's spread is $0.00588$ against $0.01135$, so the same excess reads at $+1.81\sigma$ in the depths and $+0.91$ in the heights.*** ⇒ *** AND THE CONTROL LANDS ON THE SKY'S TROUGH DEPTHS AT $-0.05\sigma$ WHILE THE ARM SITS $+1.76$ ABOVE: THE FIRST STATISTIC IN THIS SECTOR WHOSE RESIDUAL IS NOT SHARED WITH THE CONTROL *** --- against the fourth peak's $0.83\sigma$ with both models high of the sky.\rcpt{P15_the_contrast_excess_is_symmetric_about_the_envelope_and_the_troughs_are_where_the_sky_measures_it_best} ⌗ *The code seat's own framing, which is the right one: **the conclusion stands and the argument does not.*** &#9670; ⌗ ***THE GUARDS, AND ONE OF THEM RETIRES A WORRY THE ORDER CARRIED.*** *The window bias does NOT exist for this statistic --- the anchored depth is identical over the admissible windows on the sky and on both arms, **because it reads a VALUE at a located extremum rather than the LOCATION of one**, which is exactly where `cc66.45`'s parabola apex drifted six multipoles. The two statistics fail differently.* ⚠ ***But `PO-47`'s trap reproduces exactly, on depths instead of positions:*** *a free extremum search under noise returns $0.0369$ against the anchored $0.00588$, six times worse, because it latches onto noise minima. **The anchoring is necessary and not a convenience, and it is gated so the next reader cannot skip it.*** ⌗ *One real systematic, reported and not minimised: the envelope's abscissa runs the sky's depth $0.2790\to0.2830$ as the acoustic scale that sets it goes $298\to305$, about a third of a sigma.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND A CANDIDATE OFFERED UNASKED, CARRIED HERE AS A RUN AND NOT A RESULT:*** *the amplitude excess is symmetric, grows with wavenumber, and survives every localisation attempt --- and the one thing which multiplies an oscillation symmetrically about its envelope without moving its phase is **the transfer's own normalisation across the visibility**, not a phase and not a scale. The handle named is the source's conformal-time dependence across the window, which `cc66.42`'s injection replaced and `cc66.43` named as the un-measured piece.* ⛔ ***BOUND, CARRIED: $1.8\sigma$ is not a detection.*** *It is $1.8$ of the sky's own spread on ONE statistic, and the same differencing at the fourth peak gave $0.83$.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, UNCHANGED IN SUBSTANCE AND SHARPER IN FORM:** *whether the two-rate assignment is right.* ⌗ *What `cc66.43` adds is that the rule as stated **does not determine** the visibility's clock, that the instrument's two plasma-accumulated objects are already on opposite clocks, and that the peak positions support the choice it currently makes.* ⌗ *And what `cc66.44` adds is the arbiter's own resolution --- the comb pins the clock only to $\pm0.24$ of the family --- and that **the sky's value is not in the family at all**, so discharging this row cannot come from choosing between the two clocks: neither reaches the first peak the sky has.* ⌗ *And what `cc66.45` adds is that the fourth peak --- the sharpest external-referent residual this sector had --- is four fifths the control's, that what remains is one near-constant multipole inside the sky's own spread, and that the instrument locating it is good to three hundredths: **so discharging this row cannot come from the comb's residual either.*** ⌗ *And `cc66.46` leaves the row with one live handle rather than none: the excess is an amplitude excess symmetric about the envelope, the clock cannot be read from the trough depths any better than from $\theta_D/\theta_*$, and **what the sky can actually see is the depths --- $1.8\sigma$ of its own spread, and the only part of this sector's residual the control does not reproduce.*** ⌗ ⛭⛭ *And `cc66.47` gives the row the first channel it has that carries a **measured** share rather than none: the transfer's own normalisation across the visibility window, whose $\eta$-profile is now measured on both arms as a function. Through the instrument's own two-coefficient response the arm's window narrowing delivers **$23$ to $45$ per cent of the band contrast excess above $q=1.9$**, and imposing the arm's spread on the control closes $20$ to $39$ per cent of it --- the same answer by a different road, the narrowing for all of it being a factor $2.7$ above what the arm has.* ⇒ ⛔ *But it cannot be the whole of it, and the reason comes from `cc66.46` rather than from any size estimate: **the excess is symmetric about the envelope and this channel is peak-weighted by a factor of five and a half** --- $81$ per cent of the peak-height excess against $14$ per cent of the trough-depth excess. ⌗ *And the window's phase spread is nearly equal on the two arms for a reason worth recording on the row: the arm's visibility is $14.6$ per cent wider in conformal time while its phase accumulates $13.7$ per cent slower per unit of it, so **the two-rate structure reaches the window and then cancels inside it to one part in ninety.*** ⌗ *What remains un-tested and is named as such is the term mix --- the arm holding more of its window's power in the monopole in every band --- which is the one part of a normalisation difference that is not a smearing and so need not vanish at long wavelength, where the excess's own $q$-independent offset of $1.040$ sits.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND r6975 (66) LANDS THE PAPER-SIDE CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS THE FIRST DEFINITE SHARE THIS SECTOR HAS MEASURED --- AND THE FIRST REFUTATION THAT DOES NOT DEPEND ON A SIZE.*** *`P15` now carries the weighting as a real channel: read two ways that share no machinery --- the instrument's own two-coefficient response, giving a quarter to a half of the band excess above the damping scale, and the direct substitution, closing a fifth to two fifths of it --- **about a third, measured rather than modelled, from a quantity nobody chose**.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND IT CANNOT BE THE WHOLE OF IT FOR A REASON INDEPENDENT OF EVERY ONE OF THOSE SIZES, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS A REFUTATION AND NOT A SHORTFALL.*** *`cc66.46` measured the excess **symmetric about the envelope**, heights and depths carrying it equally. The weighting is not: imposed at the arm's own size it delivers **four fifths of the peak-height excess and one seventh of the trough-depth excess, a five-fold asymmetry against a symmetric object**.* ⇒ *** A PEAK-WEIGHTED CHANNEL CANNOT CARRY A SYMMETRIC EXCESS ALONE, WHATEVER ITS SHARE --- AND THAT ARGUMENT SURVIVES ANY REVISION OF THE SHARE. *** ⌷ *Two further readings agree without being needed: the measured response extrapolates to a non-zero offset at zero wavenumber, where a window's own characteristic function is identically one; and a weighting large enough to deliver the whole excess moves the first peak by eighteen multipoles and the heights by nearly half.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE SEAT'S OWN PRE-REGISTRATION WAS WRONG IN ONE DIRECTION AND SAYS SO:*** *`PREDICTION.md` argued the contrast responds between $f$ and $f^{2}$; it responds $2.3$ to $26$ times MORE, at both coefficients and in every band, **and the condition passed only because its tolerance was written on $f$ rather than on $f-1$**. Both wrong readings are banked and gated rather than deleted.* &#9670; ⇒⇒ ***SO THE ROW'S OBJECT NARROWS FOR THE SIXTH TIME AND IS NOW THE SYMMETRIC PART:*** *a share is measured, the channel that carries it is peak-weighted, and what remains is the part of the excess that is not.* ⌗ ⛭⛭ *And `cc66.48` swapped it, and the answer turns the row from a hunt for channels into a question about how they compose. **The term mix has exactly the shape the offset needs — its response is $q$-INDEPENDENT, an intercept of $1.0805$ at $q=0$ against a slope worth three per cent of it, which is the first channel measured with that signature** — and the difference is a single scalar on the Doppler, the coefficient matching the arm's monopole fraction coming out between $0.860$ and $0.897$ in all seven bands.* ⇒ ⛔ *But it **over-delivers**, raising the control's contrast by $1.10$ to $2.77$ times the whole measured excess; it is **peak-weighted at $2.23$** where the remainder it was to be is $0.49$; and it moves $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ outside the sky's locating widths, $\ell_1$ in the opposite direction from the arm.* ⇒ ⛔⛭⛭ ***So the two channels do not add: the window's third and the term mix's $1.6$ times make $1.72$ times what is measured, and the "remainder" — a RATIO of two responses — is a construct of that composition rule rather than a residual channel. What this row now needs is a composition rule and not another candidate.*** ⌗ *Two things the code seat reported against itself and which the row should carry: the anchored statistic's baseline for a SYMMETRIC operation is $1.95$ and not $1$, so the anchored reading CONFIRMS `cc66.46`'s variance split rather than contradicting it — a note of disagreement retracted before the runs landed; and its own reading of the Doppler as a smooth `loading` term was refuted by its own swap, the Doppler's band-to-band power tracking the monopole's at correlation $0.95$ and so being an oscillating term in quadrature.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND r6975 (66) LANDS THE PAPER-SIDE CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS THE FIRST DEFINITE SHARE THIS SECTOR HAS MEASURED --- AND THE FIRST REFUTATION THAT DOES NOT DEPEND ON A SIZE.*** *`P15` now carries the weighting as a real channel: read two ways that share no machinery --- the instrument's own two-coefficient response, giving a quarter to a half of the band excess above the damping scale, and the direct substitution, closing a fifth to two fifths of it --- **about a third, measured rather than modelled, from a quantity nobody chose**.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND IT CANNOT BE THE WHOLE OF IT FOR A REASON INDEPENDENT OF EVERY ONE OF THOSE SIZES, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS A REFUTATION AND NOT A SHORTFALL.*** *`cc66.46` measured the excess **symmetric about the envelope**, heights and depths carrying it equally. The weighting is not: imposed at the arm's own size it delivers **four fifths of the peak-height excess and one seventh of the trough-depth excess, a five-fold asymmetry against a symmetric object**.* ⇒ *** A PEAK-WEIGHTED CHANNEL CANNOT CARRY A SYMMETRIC EXCESS ALONE, WHATEVER ITS SHARE --- AND THAT ARGUMENT SURVIVES ANY REVISION OF THE SHARE. *** ⌷ *Two further readings agree without being needed: the measured response extrapolates to a non-zero offset at zero wavenumber, where a window's own characteristic function is identically one; and a weighting large enough to deliver the whole excess moves the first peak by eighteen multipoles and the heights by nearly half.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE SEAT'S OWN PRE-REGISTRATION WAS WRONG IN ONE DIRECTION AND SAYS SO:*** *`PREDICTION.md` argued the contrast responds between $f$ and $f^{2}$; it responds $2.3$ to $26$ times MORE, at both coefficients and in every band, **and the condition passed only because its tolerance was written on $f$ rather than on $f-1$**. Both wrong readings are banked and gated rather than deleted.* &#9670; ⇒⇒ ***SO THE ROW'S OBJECT NARROWS FOR THE SIXTH TIME AND IS NOW THE SYMMETRIC PART:*** *a share is measured, the channel that carries it is peak-weighted, and what remains is the part of the excess that is not.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r6983 (66) GATES IT, AND WHAT IT LANDS IS A CHANGE OF OBJECT RATHER THAN ANOTHER ELIMINATION: THE ROW NEEDS A COMPOSITION RULE AND NOT A FURTHER CANDIDATE.*** *Two channels are now each measured to carry a definite share, and **their shares do not add to what is observed but to $1.72$ times it** --- a third from the window and above one and a half from the term mix.* ⇒ *** SO THE TWO CANNOT BOTH BE PRESENT AT THEIR MEASURED SIZES AND SIMPLY COMPOSE; AND THE "REMAINDER" LEFT BY DIVIDING ONE RESPONSE INTO THE EXCESS IS A RATIO OF TWO RESPONSES, HENCE A CONSTRUCT OF WHATEVER THE COMPOSITION RULE TURNS OUT TO BE RATHER THAN A RESIDUAL CHANNEL WAITING TO BE IDENTIFIED. *** ⌷ ***That retracts the framing of `r6975`'s own order***, *which asked for the remainder to be characterised and for what carries it to be hunted --- **the remainder is not a thing until a composition rule exists, and cc66 answered that rather than filling the order as written**, which is the right way for an order to be wrong.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE BASELINE NOBODY HAD COMPUTED IS THE ITEM WITH THE WIDEST REACH, BECAUSE IT CHANGES HOW EVERY CLASS READING IN THIS SECTOR IS INTERPRETED.*** *The anchored contrast is read against a running-mean envelope **recomputed for each spectrum**, so a purely symmetric change of amplitude does not read as unity on it: applied to the control's own spectrum, amplitude and envelope both read $1.96$, loading $0.63$, smearing $-5.28$, **and the near-two is stable across a fivefold range of sizes**.* ⇒ **So the baseline against which a class is identified is that value and not one.** ⌷ ***And read against it, the two instruments that looked in tension agree:*** *$2.20$ against $1.96$ is twelve per cent, so the anchored reading **confirms** the band-variance split rather than contradicting it --- the same decomposition reached twice. ⌗ *`P15` now carries that baseline beside the numbers it licenses, because without it none of them can be read.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE SECOND CHANNEL IS THE FIRST WITH THE OFFSET'S OWN SHAPE, WHICH IS WHY IT WAS WORTH SWAPPING:*** *the term mix's response is **independent of wavenumber** --- an intercept against a slope worth three per cent of it --- where a window's smearing must die at long wavelength. **It is refuted as the remainder on weighting and on size and on the comb, and it is still the only channel measured to have the right shape**, which is a different state from the five eliminations before it.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND ONE IDENTIFICATION WAS REFUTED BY ITS OWN AUTHOR IN THE SAME REVISION, WITH THE MECHANISM:*** *the Doppler was argued to fill the oscillation and so to read as the loading class near $0.6$; it reads $2.23$, **because its band-to-band power tracks the monopole's at correlation $0.95$ --- an oscillating term in quadrature rather than a smooth additive one**, so removing it is nearly a pure amplitude change.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND r6983 MEASURED THE COMPOSITION RULE ITSELF, WHICH IS WHAT THE ROW HAD BECOME, AND THE MEASUREMENT CORRECTS THE REVISION THAT ASKED FOR IT.*** *Both channels imposed on ONE spectrum at the sizes their own revisions solved, neither re-chosen: the pair lands within the pre-registered $0.005$ of the product in seven of seven bands and of the sum in seven of seven, and of **quadrature in none** --- and the weighting it predicts, $2.83$, comes back as $2.80$ on an axis where quadrature predicts nothing at all.* ⌗ *Product and sum were declared inseparable here before the run and are not separated, which is a stated limit rather than a failure.* &#9670; ⛔ ***So the earlier inference is RETRACTED and its arithmetic CONFIRMED:*** *the rule is the one that revision assumed, so dividing one response into the excess is the right operation and the remainder is a well-defined object --- **the over-delivery was evidence about the SIZES and not about the RULE**; and composing the pair in one spectrum reads $1.70$ times the measured excess where adding two separate shares read $1.72$.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND WHAT SURVIVES IS A SHAPE NO COEFFICIENT CAN REMOVE, which is a stronger negative than the one it replaces:*** *the pair is flat in wavenumber --- $0.004$ of its intercept, against the measured excess's $0.442$ --- so it over-delivers by $3.87$ times at the longest wavelength and $1.38$ at the shortest. **Scale it to the offset and it is short at high wavenumber; scale it to high wavenumber and it is an order too large at the offset.** That reading is available only because the rule was measured rather than assumed.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND r6993 TURNED THE ROW FROM ELIMINATION TO SEARCH, AND THE FIRST SEARCH RETURNED EMPTY.*** *The object is no longer which channel carries the excess but **what carries its own WAVENUMBER DEPENDENCE** --- the first POSITIVE criterion this row has had, every prior step having been an elimination.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE TARGET'S SHAPE EXCLUDES THE FORM THE ROW HAS BEEN WRITTEN IN:*** *a rise linear in the squared wavenumber is refuted at more than four times its own tolerance, the only form of six excluded --- and it is the parametrisation every result in this sector quotes its variation statistic in. **The excess DECELERATES.** The concave family all fit and none is preferred; no scale is resolved; and the rise is a PREFERENCE over flatness and not an exclusion of it.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE FILTER'S FIRST APPLICATION IS EMPTY:*** *the target's departure grows three and a half times across the measured range with negative curvature, and every candidate already measured fails --- six source terms, the dipole-to-monopole ratio, and all three measured channels, the largest reaching less than half. **So the carrier is not among the things this sector has measured**, which is a statement about the list searched and the list is now written down with a number beside each entry.* &#9670; ⚠ *And the instrument was controlled before the thing it filters: a known dependence reads back to one per cent, the estimator manufactures a per-cent band wobble so a CONSTANT contrast reads as varying, and an alternative anchoring that would have overturned the premise is excluded with its mechanism rather than its number.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r6997 (66, on node cc66's cc66.50) GATES THE FIRST POSITIVE SEARCH THIS ROW HAS EVER RUN, AND IT COMES BACK EMPTY --- WHICH IS A STRONGER STATEMENT THAN ANY OF THE ELEVEN ELIMINATIONS BEFORE IT, BECAUSE IT IS ABOUT A LIST AND THE LIST IS WRITTEN DOWN.*** &#9670; ⌷ ***THE SEAT INVERTED THE ORDER AND WAS RIGHT TO:*** *the control on the filter ran **first**, because whether the statistic's wavenumber dependence belongs to the effect or to the anchoring is a question about the INSTRUMENT, and its answer is what the pre-registration's tolerances have to be written on. **An order's numbering is not a dependency graph, and the seat read the graph.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE INSTRUMENT MANUFACTURES THE THING A WEAKER READING WOULD HAVE REPORTED:*** *six forms injected into the control's own spectrum, two of them carrying a scale, recover to about a per cent --- **but the estimator's bias varies band to band by about a per cent while the six forms agree with each other on it seven times more closely.*** ⇒ *** SO A CONSTANT CONTRAST READS AS A WOBBLE, AND BAND ORDERING AT THE PER-CENT LEVEL IS A PROPERTY OF THE STATISTIC AND NOT OF THE SPECTRUM. *** ⌗ ***And the alternative anchoring that would have been the headline is excluded by its MECHANISM and not its number:*** *a median envelope returns three times the value, and the tell is that **it does not move with its own smoothing window** --- at short wavelength a running median collapses onto the curve it is meant to envelope, the median of a locally monotone stretch being its central value whatever the window. *So the rise is real and the wobble is not.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE RISE DECELERATES, WHICH EXCLUDES THE FORM EVERY RESULT IN THIS SECTOR IS WRITTEN IN.*** *A departure growing linearly in the squared wavenumber is rejected --- **the only one of six** --- and it is the parametrisation `cc66.47`, `cc66.48` and `cc66.49` all quote their variation statistic in. The concave family all fit and **none is preferred**, so the direction is settled and the form is not; **no scale is resolved**; and the rise is a PREFERENCE over flatness rather than an exclusion of it, a constant being a worse description and not an impossible one.* ⌗ *The consequence for this row's own earlier numbers is landed in `P15`: **a slope read against an intercept is a description and not a model**, and the growth of the departure is the scale-free quantity a comparison across candidates needs. The seat rebuilt the filter statistic for exactly that reason, the sector's own version reading twenty times larger for the same shape at a lower level.* ⌗ *And the pre-registration's rule as written **caught the seat's own code**, which had asked only that a central value be inside the interval where the rule required the interval itself to be.* &#9670; ⇒⇒ ***AND THE FILTER'S FIRST APPLICATION RETURNS EMPTY, WITH TWO TEETH RATHER THAN ONE:*** *the target's departure grows by a factor of three and a half **with negative curvature**. **Every candidate this sector has measured fails the first tooth** --- six source terms, the dipole-to-monopole ratio, and all three channels with measured shares.* ⇒ *** SO THE CARRIER IS NOT AMONG THE THINGS THIS SECTOR HAS MEASURED. THAT IS A STATEMENT ABOUT THE LIST SEARCHED, IT IS REPORTED AS ONE, AND THE LIST IS WRITTEN DOWN WITH A NUMBER BESIDE EVERY ENTRY. *** ⌗ *Nothing was run: every number read from banks already on disk, **which is what makes a filter cheaper than the runs it exists to save** --- the point of ordering it ahead of a third candidate.* &#9670; ⚠ ***AND ONE DISCREPANCY IS NAMED AND NOT SETTLED, WHICH MOVES A CLAIM OUT OF `P15`:*** *this row recorded the dipole-to-monopole ratio as two per cent **above** the control and rising; on the other solver path at each arm's own visibility peak it reads fourteen per cent **below** and flat. **That is not a refutation of the row --- the two may differ by the definition of the peak or by the path's treatment of the line of sight --- but the paper may not assert one of them while the other stands unreconciled.*** ⇒ *`P15` now states the disagreement, names which quantity each path measured as owed, and rests the trough-filling comparison on the visibility width and the loading, which do not depend on it.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND r6997 RECONCILED THE TWO DIPOLE READINGS AND FOUND THEM TO BE DIFFERENT QUANTITIES, WHICH REVERSED THIS SEAT'S OWN DISMISSAL OF THE CANDIDATE.*** *Both are reproduced from banks: the oscillation-amplitude ratio is two per cent ABOVE the control on its own range and still above on the shorter one, where the integrated band-power ratio is twelve per cent below --- **so the sign is the quantity and not the range**. And the one the trough-filling argument and the contrast filter both need is the AMPLITUDE, because what fills a trough is an oscillating part and the statistic is a deviation of the oscillation --- so the previous revision filtered that candidate on the wrong object.* &#9670; ⌷ ***SO THE SECOND TOOTH WAS USED AND THE CANDIDATE PASSES BOTH WHERE IT CAN BE TESTED:*** *it grows from about nothing and decelerates with the target's own curvature sign. **But it cannot be closed**: the right quantity bottoms out at half the measured range, verified stable across four window widths so the estimator is sound and simply does not reach, and the target's growth is concentrated exactly where it cannot be measured --- three and a half fold over the whole range against half that over the shared part.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND THE STEP OUTSIDE THE LIST: A WHOLE CLASS IS SETTLED BY STRUCTURE AND THE PROJECTION HOLDS AN UNIMPOSED WIDTH.*** *Every two-rate clock quantity is stored against conformal time alone, so it has the same value at every wavenumber and **cannot carry a wavenumber dependence at all** --- no run needed. And the window has TWO widths that are one object only in a one-rate cosmology: the acoustic phase varies across it by the leaf horizon's width, which is what the window channel acted on, while the projection integrates over the width in conformal time. **The arm's window is six and a half per cent wider in the one and slightly narrower in the other --- a ratio seven per cent different from the control's, and nothing has ever imposed it.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r6999 (66, on node cc66's cc66.51) REVERSES `cc66.50`'s DISMISSAL OF A CANDIDATE ON THE SEAT'S OWN ERROR RATHER THAN ON NEW DATA, AND THE ROW NOW HAS EXACTLY ONE CANDIDATE STANDING AND ONE STRUCTURAL FINDING NOBODY HAD LOOKED FOR.*** &#9670; ⛔⛭ ***THE SIGN WAS NOT THE RANGE, IT WAS THE QUANTITY --- AND BOTH READINGS REPRODUCE FROM BANKS RATHER THAN BEING ARGUED ABOUT.*** *This row's recorded figure is a ratio of **oscillation amplitudes** and reads $+2.06$ per cent over its own range --- **exactly the registered value** --- and still $+0.95$ over the part of the other range it reaches. `cc66.50` filtered a ratio of **band-integrated powers**, which reads $-11.58$. **They differ in what is ratioed, in which fields, and over what range.*** ⇒ *** SO THERE WAS NEVER A DISAGREEMENT ABOUT ONE QUANTITY, AND THE ONE THE ARGUMENT NEEDS IS THE AMPLITUDE RATIO --- WHICH IS PHYSICS AND NOT A CONVENTION: TROUGH-FILLING IS AN OSCILLATING TERM A QUARTER PERIOD OUT OF PHASE, SO WHAT FILLS A TROUGH IS AN OSCILLATING AMPLITUDE WHILE THE SMOOTH PART DISPLACES THE ENVELOPE AND FILLS NOTHING; AND THE CONTRAST STATISTIC IS ITSELF A DEVIATION OF THE OSCILLATION ABOUT A RUNNING MEAN. *** ⌗ ***So `cc66.50`'s filter row answered the wrong question, and the seat said so in those terms.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE SECOND TOOTH IS USED, BECAUSE SOMETHING SURVIVED THE FIRST.*** *On the range where the right quantity can be measured, the candidate's departure grows and **decelerates**, its curvature of the same sign as the target's and of comparable size. **It passes both conditions everywhere it can be tested.*** &#9670; ⚠ ***AND A STATISTIC WAS RETURNING A NUMBER OUTSIDE ITS DOMAIN, WHICH IS THE NEAR-MISS WORTH RECORDING:*** *the departure crosses zero inside the range, so the growth ratio is not a ratio of anything there --- **and the arithmetic returned a large negative value that would have read as a spectacular pass.** The statistic now carries its domain.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND YET THE FILTER CANNOT CLOSE IT, WHICH IS A RESULT ABOUT THE INSTRUMENT AND NOT A GAP:*** *an oscillation amplitude needs about a period either side of where it is estimated, so the acoustic period sets a floor the estimator does not reach --- **stable to a tenth of a per cent across four window widths, so the estimator is sound and simply stops.** And the excess's growth is **concentrated exactly where that ratio cannot be measured**: three and a half fold over the whole range against under one and a half over the shared part.* ⇒ **What would settle it is a different estimator below that floor, not a longer run of this one.** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND ONE WHOLE CLASS IS EXCLUDED BY STRUCTURE AND NEEDS NO RUN AT ALL:*** *every quantity belonging to the two clocks --- the leaf clock, the ruler, the Jacobian between them, the visibility, the optical depth --- is stored against **conformal time alone**, and a function of conformal time has the same value at every wavenumber, so **it cannot vary across one**. ⌗ *Neither measured-and-failed nor unmeasured: **cannot carry it, by the shape of the object.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND THE PROJECTION HOLDS A WIDTH NOTHING HAS EVER IMPOSED, WHICH IS WHERE THIS ROW'S OWN NAME HAS POINTED SINCE IT OPENED.*** *Two widths govern the window and they are not the same width: **the acoustic phase swept across it** goes with the width in the leaf sound horizon, which is what `cc66.47` acted on, while **the projection's smearing** goes with the width in conformal time, that being what the projection's argument carries. *In a one-rate cosmology these are one object up to the sound speed; on two rates they are not, because the clocks differ.* ⇒ *** THE ARM'S WINDOW IS SOME SIX PER CENT WIDER IN CONFORMAL TIME AND SLIGHTLY NARROWER IN THE LEAF HORIZON, A RATIO SEVEN PER CENT DIFFERENT FROM THE CONTROL'S, AND NOTHING IN THE COMPARISON HAS EVER IMPOSED IT. *** ⌗ *Found by asking what was outside the list rather than by running anything, which is what the order asked for and is the cheapest finding in the sector's history.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE ROW'S DISCHARGE NARROWS TO TWO THINGS:*** *an estimator below the period floor, and that unimposed width. ⌗ *Nothing was run: the order's last clause, kept --- and no pre-registration, **with the reason stated rather than skipped**, the only criterion applied being one fixed in `r6993`'s own pre-registration before the measurement it is applied to.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r7001 (66, on node cc66's cc66.52) TURNS THE UNIMPOSED WIDTH INTO A CR PREDICTION AND THEN BOUNDS THE CHANNEL IT OPENS OUT OF CONTENTION --- BOTH IN ONE REVISION, AND THE ORDER SAID THE FIRST WAS WORTH MORE.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***THE DIFFERENCE IS FORCED, AND BY ONE OBJECT.*** *The two sound horizons on one integrand and two rates are related **pointwise by the Jacobian between the rates**, checked across the window to **one part in a million** on the arm and exactly on the control, where it is unity by the rate identity.* ⌷ ***AND THE RULER CLOCK IS WHAT PROVES IT RATHER THAN AN ASSERTION:*** *against that clock **the two arms are the same instrument**, their window widths agreeing to $0.3$ per cent on both core definitions --- the visibility being laid down in conformal time by Thomson scattering on the physical background, **which is the same physics on both arms**. Against the leaf clock it is $+14.5$ per cent **and the whole of that is the Jacobian**, the arm's own leaf-to-ruler ratio reproducing the Jacobian's mean to $0.4$ per cent.* ⇒ *** SO THE DIFFERENT RATIO IS A PREDICTION OF THE TWO-RATE ASSIGNMENT: THE JACOBIAN CARRIES NO FREE COEFFICIENT, IS FIXED BY THE BACKGROUND SOLUTION ONCE THE ARM IS SPECIFIED, AND IS IDENTICALLY ONE ON ANY ONE-RATE COSMOLOGY. *** &#9670; ⚠ ***AND IT CORRECTS `cc66.51`'s NUMBER, WHICH THIS SEAT HAD ALREADY LANDED:*** *a width is not a quantity until its definition is named. **Root-mean-square gives $+7.3$ per cent --- the figure landed at `r6999` --- while full width at half maximum and the characteristic function's half-fall both give $+14.5$ and agree with each other to better than a hundredth of a per cent.** The window has long tails, the root-mean-square weights them, and **neither the projection nor the phase sweep responds to them**. ⌗ *The $7.3$ is not wrong; it is the tail-weighted reading of the same object --- and `P15` now names the definition.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE ORDER'S FIRST ITEM CANNOT BE RUN AS WRITTEN, WHICH FOLLOWS FROM THE FINDING RATHER THAN FROM EFFORT:*** *"the arm's conformal width at the same leaf width" **is** "the arm's Jacobian", and a control carrying the Jacobian is not a control. **There is no knob for the projection width and the instrument is right not to have one.** Reported rather than substituted for --- and the pre-registration was written anyway, all four outcomes with the null named, **because the guard is about what was claimable in advance and not about whether the run happened.*** &#9670; ⛔⛭ ***AND THE FILTER APPLIED IN THE KERNEL CORRECTS THE SEAT'S OWN FIRST PASS BY A FACTOR OF FIFTEEN AND IN SIGN.*** *That pass used a plane-wave proxy on the reading that the projection's argument advances at the wavenumber. **It does not**: near its turning point --- and the whole window sits there, that coincidence being what the projection IS --- the kernel oscillates at a local rate that vanishes.* ⇒ ***THE VERDICT: it delivers about a quarter of what the excess needs in the band where the excess is largest, and it ACCELERATES where the target decelerates, failing the committed second tooth under both anchorings. NOT THE CARRIER; NOT EXCLUDED AS A CONTRIBUTOR; AND ITS SIZE IS NOW BOUNDED RATHER THAN UNKNOWN.*** &#9670; ⚠ ***AND THE FILTER NEEDED A THIRD TOOTH --- THE SECOND REVISION RUNNING IN WHICH THIS STATISTIC RETURNED A NUMBER OUTSIDE ITS DOMAIN.*** *The superseded plane-wave numbers **passed both pre-registered teeth while moving the contrast the wrong way in every band**, because the growth ratio is a ratio of a departure to a departure and **is blind to their common sign**.* ⇒ **SIGN is now the first tooth and the growth is read only after it** --- *registered even though the estimate that exposed it is superseded, because it is a defect of the statistic and not of that estimate.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND THE ESTIMATOR BELOW THE PERIOD FLOOR IS SPECIFIED AND NOT BUILT, AS ASKED:*** *a two-parameter fit at the known period rather than an amplitude read off a window, needing a fraction of a period of support --- **with its cost stated, that an error of a few per cent in the held period leaks into the amplitude as exactly the slow drift being measured**, so it must be validated against the window estimator where both work before anything it says below the floor is read.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r7003 (66, on node cc66's cc66.53) BUILDS THE ESTIMATOR, REACHES A BAND NOTHING HAD REACHED, PASSES EVERY CONDITION THAT CAN BE APPLIED --- AND FINDS THAT THE ONE WHICH WOULD DECIDE CANNOT BE APPLIED AT ALL.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***FIRST, A CORRECTION TO THIS ROW'S OWN LANDED RESULT, AND THE SEAT MADE IT UNPROMPTED.*** *`cc66.51`'s curvature pass **does not survive**: read with the window estimator on its own range the candidate's curvature runs clearly negative at the width it was read at, through nearly zero, to **positive** at the widest. **`cc66.51`'s stability check had certified the MEAN over a sub-range --- the curvature was never the quantity that was checked.*** ⇒ ***A stability check licenses the statistic it was run on and no other***, *which is the same shape of error that opened `PO-65` and broke a register row on the same day. **The mean is reproduced and stands; only the curvature read off it does not.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE ESTIMATOR'S FIRST FINDING IS THAT THE PERIOD IS NOT THE PERIOD.*** *Two by construction, it runs about two per cent short in the fields **and by a different amount for the two fields** --- read off the drift of the recovered phase and iterated to self-consistency. So the held period is measured **per field and per arm** with its own uncertainty from disjoint sub-ranges.* ⌷ ***VALIDATION FIRST, AS ORDERED, AND THE OLD ESTIMATOR IS THE ONE THAT FAILS IT:*** *the means agree where both are sound, and **the window estimator does not survive its own width** --- its ripple running from under one per cent to above ten as it narrows --- while the held one sits near two and a half per cent at every width and reaches far below the other's hard floor.* ⌗ ***And the cost the seat itself named is measured at half a per cent of the dependence being measured, with a mechanism***: *the fit re-fits the phase in every window, **so a wrong held period is absorbed there and costs only a common amplitude factor, which cancels in a ratio of ratios**.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***SIGN FIRST, GROWTH AFTER --- AND IT PASSES BOTH.*** *On the unlocked range the candidate's departure is **positive in every band as the target's is**, and grows faster than the target's does. ⌗ *And it stops where the **fields** stop rather than where the method does: the lowest band's window straddles the first acoustic excursion, **where there is no oscillation amplitude to estimate at all** --- a floor of the fields and not of the method, and the pre-registration's second null.* &#9670; ⛔⛭ ***AND THE CONDITION THAT WOULD DECIDE CANNOT BE APPLIED, FOR AN EXACT REASON RATHER THAN A STATISTICAL ONE.*** *On the full range the target's curvature is what `r6993` pre-registered. **Dropping the lowest band ALONE flips its sign; dropping any other single band leaves it where it was.*** ⇒ *** THE TARGET'S DECELERATION IS CARRIED ENTIRELY BY THE ONE BAND NO ESTIMATOR OF THE CANDIDATE CAN REACH. THE FILTER IS OUT OF CONDITIONS RATHER THAN THE CANDIDATE OUT OF CHANCES --- A STATEMENT ABOUT THE FILTER, AND THE PRE-REGISTRATION'S FIRST NULL. *** ⌗ *The candidate's own curvature is stable there; **what is missing is the target's sign and not the candidate's number.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE WIDTH RATIO IS A SECOND AND INDEPENDENT READING OF THE JACOBIAN RATHER THAN A RESTATEMENT OF THE COMB, WHICH IS WORTH MORE THAN THE OBSERVABILITY QUESTION IT WAS ASKED UNDER.*** *The comb reads the **cumulative** ratio at last scattering --- which is what sets the peak spacing this paper already matches, the alternative reading not being close --- while the widths read the **window-local average**, and the two differ by about a third.* ⇒ **So a construction tuned to the comb must still return the right LOCAL Jacobian to match the widths: a second demand on the same object, neither carrying a free coefficient and both identically one on a single rate.** &#9670; ⛔ ***AND IT IS NOT OBSERVABLE IN ITS OWN RIGHT, WHICH THE SEAT ANSWERED AGAINST ITS OWN INTEREST:*** *the width the damping reads differs between the arms by under a per cent where the width the projection reads differs by more than ten, and the projection's contrast effect was already bounded at twice what it would need with its sign undetermined. ⇒ *So by the order's own instruction **the passage stays where it is**, and the independence finding is routed marked as NOT an observability argument.* ⌗ *`cc66.53`, 23 gates, all pass; pre-registration with **both** nulls tabled and the date of each of its own parts stated.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r7005 (66, on node cc66's cc66.54) CLOSES THE BAND EXACTLY, ANSWERS THE ROBUSTNESS QUESTION IN BOTH DIRECTIONS AT ONCE, AND FINDS THE FILTER BROKEN IN THE WORST POSSIBLE PLACE.*** &#9670; ⛔⛭ ***THE SEAT LED WITH WHAT WORKS AGAINST ITS OWN RESULT, AND IT MOVES A CLAIM THIS SEAT LANDED.*** *Within the contrast statistic as defined the excess's curvature **is** robust --- negative under every variation of the envelope width, the band edges and the sampling. **But a running MEDIAN envelope, same window, same bands, everything else unchanged, REVERSES it.*** ⌗ *That is a different statistic and the difference is measured --- the median absorbs about half the top band's oscillation on both arms, so its ratios form on a much smaller residual --- **but it is not on its face the worse one: it leaves a departure of zero mean where the arithmetic one does not.*** ⇒ *** SO THE SIGN OF THE CURVATURE BELONGS TO THE STATISTIC AND NOT YET TO THE EXCESS, AND `P15` NOW SAYS SO RATHER THAN CHOOSING AN ENVELOPE. *** &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE ANSWER TO "IS IT LANDED TOO STRONGLY" IS PRECISE AND IS NOT THE ONE THE ORDER EXPECTED:*** *the claim is supported and **it is a single-band claim** --- the lowest band's own value is stable and with it dropped the sign is not determined, **so the deceleration is that band lying LOW rather than the upper bands bending**. ⇒ *So not "landed too strongly": **what was landed too strongly is the claim's INDEPENDENCE** --- of any one band and of the envelope's definition --- *neither of which the paper stated and a reader would assume both.* **Both are now stated.** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE BAND IS CLOSED BY THE FIELDS AT AN EXACT FLOOR, WITH THE CRITERION NAMED BEFORE USE AND NECESSARY RATHER THAN CHOSEN:*** *an oscillation amplitude is identified at a point only if the window holds a turning point on **each** side, because across a span with no turning point the held-period pair is monotone and the baseline polynomial already spans it. **The monopole has no turning point below its first --- the first excursion is one-sided --- and the monopole BINDS**, the floor sitting far above the lowest band while every other band lies entirely above it.* ⌷ ***And it is the fields' floor and not the window's, shown three ways:*** *width --- the spread is four times larger below it than above and changes sign; placement --- sweeping the edge, the departure is negative for every window opening past the monopole's first turning point and positive for every one staying above, **what lies below being the field's rise from its initial condition rather than an oscillation**; and prediction --- every centre below the floor spreads more than every centre above, **which the floor was not fitted to**.* ⇒ *** NO ESTIMATOR OF AN OSCILLATION AMPLITUDE CAN REACH INSIDE THE FIRST EXCURSION: AN ACOUSTIC OSCILLATION HAS A FIRST EXTREMUM, AND BEFORE IT THERE IS NO AMPLITUDE BECAUSE THERE IS NO OSCILLATION. *** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE THIRD CONDITION IS NOT UNAVAILABLE IN GENERAL, WHICH IS WORSE THAN IF IT WERE.*** *It needs a value in the closed band --- and that band is shut to **amplitude** estimators but not to a candidate computed from the projection's kernel, which had one, and which the condition duly excluded.* ⇒ *** SO THE FILTER'S FULL STRENGTH IS AVAILABLE EXACTLY FOR THE CLASS THAT CANNOT CARRY THE EXCESS, AND ITS THIRD CONDITION IS PERMANENTLY UNAVAILABLE EXACTLY FOR THE CLASS THAT CAN --- BECAUSE WHAT FILLS A TROUGH IS AN OSCILLATING AMPLITUDE, WHICH IS WHAT CLOSES THE BAND. *** &#9670; ⌷ ***AND IS A TWO-CONDITION FILTER A FILTER? THE ANSWER IS THAT IT IS WHAT IT ALWAYS WAS.*** *As an **exclusion** device it has lost nothing --- each remaining condition is necessary and **every exclusion this sector ever made was made on growth alone**. As a **confirmation** device it was never one, with three conditions or with two: all three are conditions on SHAPE, and the surviving candidate matches sign and growth direction while being several times too small at the top band.* ⇒ **So passing two is worth what passing three would have been, the candidate is not excluded, and the row's remaining question is the COUPLING** --- *quantitative, living where the candidate is measured, and needing the closed band not at all.* ⌗ *Named as the question rather than proposed as a channel; pre-registration with the FAILURE MODES tabled ahead of the outcomes, on this revision's own new guard.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND r7007 (66, on node cc66's cc66.55) MEASURES THE COUPLING, FINDS IT FIXED BY THE CONSTRUCTION, AND THEN CANNOT CLOSE THE CONTRIBUTION --- BECAUSE THE TWO READINGS OF WHAT THE COUPLING MULTIPLIES DISAGREE IN **SIGN**.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***THE COUPLING CAME OUT CLEAN AND ITS SIGN IS UNANIMOUS:*** *three banked spectra give the response as a finite difference --- **three points, so linearity is checked rather than assumed** --- and it steepens towards the unscaled value, so the slope there is both the applicable one and the one over the shortest step. **Negative at every band, on both envelopes, over every interval: forty-two readings of the sign and not one positive.*** ⌗ *Which is `cc66.51`'s own trough-filling physics read **forwards**: more of an oscillating term in quadrature fills more trough and LOWERS the contrast.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***AND THE CONSTRUCTION FIXES IT, SHOWN BY WHAT FAILS TO CHANGE IT:*** *the control's own ratio of that term's band power to the monopole's runs across **a factor of more than six** from the lowest band to the highest, **and the coupling's sign is the same at every one**.* ⇒ *** A QUANTITY WHOSE SIGN IS INVARIANT ACROSS A FACTOR OF SIX IN THE VERY RATIO IT DEPENDS ON IS NOT SET BY A KNOB: THE KNOB IS THE PROBE AND NOT THE SETTER, SO THE SIZE IS A PREDICTION AND NOT A FIT. *** ⌗ *One thing not measured and said rather than assumed: **the three banks are the control's**, so the arm's coupling is the control's transferred, and transferred only as far as the sign.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE CONTRIBUTION DOES NOT CLOSE, FOR A REASON THAT IS NOT THE COUPLING'S.*** *The criterion was named before use --- the coupling multiplies a fractional change in that term's oscillation amplitude relative to the monopole's --- **and two measured quantities both claim to be it and they disagree in SIGN.** Read in the fields at last scattering the arm is higher, making the contribution negative and the candidate unhelpful; read in the projected source as a band power the arm is lower, making it positive and of the right order to matter.* ⇒ *** SO THE COUPLING TURNS A DISAGREEMENT ABOUT A QUANTITY INTO A DISAGREEMENT ABOUT WHETHER THE CANDIDATE HELPS AT ALL --- A FACTOR OF SEVEN APART EVEN AT THEIR CLOSEST. *** ⌗ ***Third revision in which these two readings decide the answer between them, first in which they decide its SIGN --- and the seat is NOT choosing***: *`cc66.51` chose once, and this revision shows the choice decides the sign.* &#9670; ⌷ ***AND THE SHORTFALL GROWS ON BOTH READINGS, IN OPPOSITE DIRECTIONS, SO IT IS A SHAPE MISMATCH EITHER WAY --- THE FOURTH THIS SECTOR HAS FOUND.*** *The coupling is flat across wavenumber, **so whatever varies belongs to the input and not to the response.*** ⌷ ***And what would settle it is named and is neither banked quantity:*** *the first is an amplitude but at last scattering rather than in the projected source; the second is in the projected source but is a **band power**, which keeps the smooth part that fills no trough. **The quantity is the oscillation amplitude of the projected contribution, band by band, and the instrument banks neither it nor the resolved fields a derivative of it would need.** One bank, not a channel.* &#9670; ✔ ***AND THE ENVELOPE QUESTION HAS THE GOOD ANSWER:*** *the coupling's sign survives **both** envelopes at every band, so **the contribution does not reverse with the statistic** and the coupling question does not inherit the envelope ambiguity. Its **magnitude** does --- the median envelope's coupling reaches more than twice the other's at the top band --- *so a quoted fraction inherits it while the sign does not, and that is said before any fraction is quoted.* ⌗ *The envelope is not revisited to settle it: the order forbade that, both are carried, neither chosen.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7009` (`cc66.56`) --- THE BANK THE LAST ORDER CALLED FOR ALREADY EXISTED, SO THE ANSWER TO "EMIT THE RIGHT QUANTITY" IS THAT NOTHING HAD TO BE EMITTED.*** *`SRCDEC`, added by this line at `r6915+cc66.41`, already writes the **full bilinear decomposition**: ten $\ell$-resolved terms per multipole summing to $C_\ell$ **exactly**, verified to $1.2\times10^{-15}$, with `r6915_pairs_{lcdm,cr}` on disk for both arms. **No run, no instrument change, and the order's escape hatch unused.*** ⌗ *And the coarse grid is **checked** rather than assumed adequate --- 238 multipoles against 1900, reading the contrast 1.9 per cent low with the deficit agreeing between the arms to $3\times10^{-4}$, **so it cancels in the ratio**, which is the only thing read from it.* &#9670; ✔ ***AND THAT BANK DISCHARGES THE CAVEAT `cc66.55` COULD ONLY STATE.*** *The $s$-scaling of every pair is exact --- $s^2$ on the Doppler square, $s$ on each of its three crosses --- so the coupling follows **ANALYTICALLY for the ARM as well as the control**: $-0.48$ to $-0.86$ on each, **equal between the arms to 4 per cent**, and agreeing with `cc66.55`'s three-point finite difference to 7.* ⇒ ***The control-transferred caveat is gone, and the same quantity is now reached two independent ways.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE CONTRIBUTION IS DETERMINED: A MINORITY SHARE THAT GROWS WITH WAVENUMBER AND IS SIGN-INDEFINITE AT THE BOTTOM.*** *Read by a **one-at-a-time swap** --- the arm's four Doppler-carrying pairs replaced by the control's, envelope-scaled, on the arm's own $q$ grid, the share being a ratio of contrast differences. **Same shape of operation as `SRCINJRS`'s clock swap, which the instrument already sanctions, and with no coupling-times-input product anywhere in it.** Arithmetic envelope: $+1, -12, +5, +12, +11, +26, +17$ per cent. Median envelope: $+12, -20, -13, +32, +33, +34, +48$.* ⇒ *** SO THE CANDIDATE IS A REAL CONTRIBUTOR AND IT IS NOT THE CARRIER --- AND IT LANDS BETWEEN THE TWO WRONG READINGS, WHICH SAID $-13$ AND $+188$ PER CENT. *** ⌗ *The order required "settles nothing" be tabled first; **it is not that outcome**, because a minority share is what neither wrong reading said. And `cc66.55`'s split is **confirmed rather than assumed** --- the coupling's sign identical on both envelopes while the fraction moves by $2.7\times$. **No envelope chosen, second revision running.*** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***AND THE FOUR SHAPE MISMATCHES DO SHARE A REASON, AND IT IS ABOUT THE EXCESS RATHER THAN ABOUT THE CANDIDATES.*** *The excess has **one dominant feature**: band 1 is $0.33$ of the mean of the rest and sits $4.9$ scatters below it, while above band 1 **a constant already fits** --- scatter $0.0088$ on a mean of $0.0645$ --- and a line only halves the residual.* ⇒ *** THE EXCESS'S $q$-STRUCTURE IS A STEP AT THE LOWEST BAND AND NOT A TREND --- AND ALL FOUR CANDIDATES WERE JUDGED ON A TREND ACROSS BANDS 2--7, WHERE THE EXCESS IS VERY NEARLY FEATURELESS. *** *A flat candidate (the window weighting, the term mix, the band-power reading) matches the flat part and misses the step; a rising one (the projection width, the field reading) matches the mild rise and misses the step; and there the two are barely distinguishable **because there is almost nothing to distinguish them with**.* ⇒ ***So this sector has been spending its discriminating power on the part of the excess carrying least structure, because the part carrying the structure is the band `cc66.54` showed it cannot measure a candidate in.*** ⌗ *Which is `cc66.54`'s own finding seen from the candidate side rather than the target's, **and it is why four mismatches look like four failures rather than one**.* ⌗ *`cc66.56`, 13 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`, with a pre-registration tabling five failure modes ahead of five outcomes and dating one failed construction as failed.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7011` (`cc66.57`) --- THE STEP IS THE EXCESS'S OWN, IT SITS AT THE SECOND ACOUSTIC PEAK TO $0.9$ PER CENT, AND ALL FOUR CANDIDATES CAN BE SCORED ON IT AND NONE PRODUCES IT.*** *And the pre-registration was written **before** anything in ⓵ was computed, saying so in terms, naming the step's definition before use, and **tabling first the outcome that would withdraw `cc66.56`'s own ⓷** --- the finding this seat had already landed in two sections.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓵ THE STEP IS THE EXCESS'S AND NOT THE ESTIMATOR'S, ON FOUR READINGS OF WHICH ONE USES NO WINDOW AT ALL.*** *Three envelopes differing in **kind** --- running arithmetic mean, running median, and a local quadratic --- and the **peak-to-trough depth per acoustic cycle**, which needs no envelope whatever. Step ratios $0.333$, $0.391$, $0.633$ and $-0.025$: **never near one on any of them**. And the envelope-free reading is the sharpest: band 1's excess on it is $-0.0017$, nothing at all, against a bands 2--7 mean of $+0.0654$ --- coarse, carrying one acoustic cycle per band, and said to be so.* ⌷ ***AND THE EDGE HAZARD THE ORDER RAISED IS RULED OUT THE RIGHT WAY ROUND, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES IT RULED OUT:*** *band 1's envelope does come within $0.018$ in $q$ --- five multipoles --- of truncating. **But moving the band's lower edge UP, away from the hazard, makes it read $+0.0215 \to +0.0361$: HIGHER, where a truncation artefact would have to make it read lower.*** ⚠ *And the step's SIZE is reading-dependent where its existence is not, so it is quoted as **band 1 between nothing and two thirds of the rest** and not as a number. **No envelope chosen --- a third added, as ordered.*** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓶ AND IT SITS AT THE SECOND ACOUSTIC PEAK.*** *The half-rise point, in sliding windows at three widths, is $q = 1.789,\,1.788,\,1.805$ --- **$1.794 \pm 0.008$** --- against the second peak at $1.7775$ on the control and $1.7760$ on the arm: **a match to $0.9$ per cent, where the nearest other scale this construction fixes is SEVEN per cent away**. And it is sharp: a clean factor of $2.0$ on all three widths, reached within about a tenth of a comb period --- **narrower than the narrowest window that resolves it**.* ⇒ *** THE EXCESS IS ONE SIZE IN THE FIRST ACOUSTIC CYCLE AND TWICE THAT IN EVERY CYCLE ABOVE IT. *** ⛔ *And the seat declines to call that a mechanism, its own pre-registration having said a step at a scale is a signature to be explained.* ⌗ ***And the scale is not the arm's:*** *the peaks sit at fixed $q$ on **both** arms, so the step says the **first acoustic cycle** behaves differently rather than that some new length has entered.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓷ AND ALL FOUR CAN BE SCORED ON THE STEP, AND NONE OF THEM PRODUCES IT.*** *The **window weighting** and the **term mix** were both measured flat in $q$, and **a flat candidate's step ratio is exactly one by construction, without a new number**. The **projection width**, kernel-class and reachable at band 1, is fitted by a straight line in $q$ to $2.6$ per cent of its own range --- linear, so no step. And the **Doppler**, by the swap route that reaches band 1, runs $-0.0016$ to $+0.0014$ in sliding half-period windows with means $-0.0001$ below the step and $-0.0002$ above: **noise at the step's resolution and no transition at all**.* ⇒ *** SO THIS IS NOT THE IDENTIFIABILITY FLOOR CLOSING OVER THE LIST --- EVERY ONE OF THE FOUR WAS EVALUABLE --- IT IS THE LIST EXHAUSTED AGAINST THE FEATURE THAT CARRIES THE STRUCTURE. *** ⌗ ***And the row's question is now narrower than it has been for six revisions: what distinguishes the first acoustic cycle from the rest?*** *Which is not a channel, and the seat proposes none.* ⌗ *`cc66.57`, 12 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`, five seconds, with the pre-registration written before the measurement.* &#9670;
