⭭⭭ ***`r7013` --- THE TWO EXITS, WRITTEN SO THIS ROW CAN END EITHER WAY, AND `r7011` PUT THE SECOND ONE WITHIN REACH.*** **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *the carrier of the contrast excess identified --- something this construction fixes that produces a **step at the second acoustic peak**, which is now the feature rather than a trend across bands.* ***OR TERMINATES IF:*** *it is demonstrated that no quantity this construction fixes can produce that step --- **and `cc66.57` has already closed the list that existed**, every one of the four evaluable against the step and none producing it.* ⇒ *** IN WHICH CASE THE TERMINAL STATE IS A MEASURED DISCREPANCY OF KNOWN SIZE, KNOWN LOCATION AND CLOSED CANDIDATE LIST, WHICH `P15` CARRIES AS A RESIDUAL WITH ITS SIZE STATED --- NOT A SECTOR STILL LOOKING. *** ⌗ *What stands between the two exits is one question and it is named: **what distinguishes the first acoustic cycle from the rest.** A row whose remaining question is a single sentence is a row near one of its two ends, and this gate's job is to make sure it can reach either.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7015` (`cc66.58`) --- THE WINDOW-FREE READING CANNOT RESOLVE THE FIRST CYCLE, THE FLATNESS WAS MEASURED AS A TREND SO THE LIST IS **NOT** EXHAUSTED, AND **NINE TENTHS OF THE STEP IS COMMON TO BOTH ARMS.*** *Pre-registration committed as its own commit **ahead of** the working script, so the ordering is in the history and not in a sentence --- and **every one of its three outcome tables leads with the row that damages `cc66.57`. Two of the three fired.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***⓵ THE GATING SEAT'S STRONGER CLAIM IS NOT ESTABLISHED, AND NOT BECAUSE THE EXCESS IS NON-ZERO --- BECAUSE THE READING IT RESTED ON HAS **ONE DATUM**.*** *The window-free contrast carries **one half-cycle transition in band 1**, the first locatable extremum pair sitting at $q = 1.363$ against a step at $1.794$. Band 1 per-transition: $-0.0008$, **$0.02\sigma$ against zero and $1.60\sigma$ against the bands 2--7 mean** --- ***consistent with zero AND with there being no step at all***, on the per-transition scatter of the ten data in the featureless stretch, which is the only honest yardstick available.* ⌗ *And the finer version does not rescue it: the **extremal envelope** covers only $27\%$ of band 1, its below-step stretch rests on the **same single extremum**, and it interpolates **across** the step so its $+0.0245$ is biased toward the above-step value. Three extremum definitions give the same below/above ratio to six decimals --- **the finder is not what decides it**.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE SEAT WITHDRAWS ITS OWN `cc66.57` DESCRIPTION, UNASKED:*** *the window-free reading was called “the sharpest of the four” and ***IT WAS THE COARSEST*** --- one datum against four hundred. **“Envelope-free” is a statement about BIAS and not about RESOLUTION, and the two were run together.*** ✔ ***And the withdrawal TIGHTENS the claim rather than loosening it***, *which is the opposite of what a withdrawal usually does: the “none” endpoint was never a reading, it was an unresolved number.* ⇒ *** BAND 1 IS BETWEEN A THIRD AND TWO THIRDS OF THE REST, ON EVERY STATISTIC THAT CAN SEE IT --- $0.333$, $0.391$, $0.633$ WINDOWED AND $0.338$ ON THE EXTREMAL ENVELOPE. *** *The step stays a step and does not become an onset; the stronger claim is not established and **not excluded**.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓶ THE RANGE WAS FULL, SO THE ORDER'S GUARD DOES NOT FIRE THERE --- BUT IT FIRES ON THE **STATISTIC**, AND THAT IS WORSE.*** *Gated on `cc66.49`'s own file rather than recalled: its fit abscissa runs all seven band centres, **so band 1 IS in the flatness measurement** and the disposal is not circular on range.* ⇒ ***The error that IS there: the flatness was measured as a TREND*** --- *slope times mean $q^2$ over intercept, from a line in $q^2$.* **A step is badly fitted by a line and shows up in the RESIDUAL, which that statistic never looked at**: *the joint reads $0.004$ on it --- “the flattest thing this sector has measured” --- **while leaving $61\%$ of its own range unexplained by that same line**.* ⇒ *** SO “A FLAT CANDIDATE'S STEP RATIO IS EXACTLY ONE, BY CONSTRUCTION AND WITHOUT A NEW NUMBER” WAS NEVER ENTAILED BY WHAT WAS MEASURED. IT NEEDED A NUMBER. *** &#9670; ⌷ ***RE-SCORED ON BAND 1'S DEPARTURE FROM ITS OWN BANDS 2--7 TREND, ACROSS FOUR BASES WITH NONE CHOSEN*** *(linear and log, in $q$ and $q^2$; a departure counts only if it survives all four).* **Window weighting $+0.45$ to $+0.56$: steps UP, the WRONG sign.** **Term mix $-0.35$ to $-0.39$: the right sign, $63 \%$ of the excess's.** **The JOINT, the realised pair --- one spectrum, both coefficients at the sizes their own profiles solve --- $-0.23$ to $-0.27$: $42\%$ of the step, clearing $2\sigma$ on three of four.** *The measured excess is $-0.566$ to $-0.601$.* ⇒ *** `cc66.57`'s ⓷ IS WITHDRAWN IN PART AND THE LIST IS **NOT** EXHAUSTED: NO CANDIDATE PRODUCES THE STEP **ALONE**, AND THE TWO CHANNELS BETWEEN THEM CARRY A MINORITY OF IT. *** *A partial account rather than none --- a minority share, which is what the Doppler turned out to be as well.* ✔ ***And the projection width's disposal STANDS, which is what the basis table is for***: *linear in $q$ to $2.6\%$ of its range, it **flips sign** across the family and a log basis would have manufactured a step for it.* ⌗ *** OF THE FOUR DISPOSALS, THE ONE DONE ON A RESIDUAL IS THE ONE THAT HOLDS AND THE TWO DONE ON A TREND ARE THE TWO THAT FAIL. *** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓷ AND THE DECOMPOSITION IS DECISIVE, AND IT IS THE BOTH-ARMS ROW.*** *Each arm's own contrast against its **own** bands 2--7 trend, no reference to the other arm, on all four bases and on both statistics: windowed, control $+0.303$ to $+0.404$ against arm $+0.268$ to $+0.360$; window-free, control $+0.364$ to $+0.435$ against arm $+0.334$ to $+0.389$.* ⇒ *** BOTH ARMS STEP, IN THE SAME DIRECTION, BY TENS OF PER CENT, ON EVERY BASIS AND BOTH STATISTICS --- AND THE ARM IS **ALWAYS** THE SHALLOWER, BY ABOUT ELEVEN PER CENT AND NO MORE. *** ⇒ *** SO ABOUT NINE TENTHS OF THE STEP IS COMMON TO THE TWO ARMS AND CANCELS IN THE RATIO. THE EXCESS'S STEP IS THE TENTH THAT DOES NOT. *** ⌗ ***And it pays a debt without a new number***: *it explains why `cc66.57` found the location at fixed $q$ on **both** arms. **That was reported as a caveat and it was a clue.*** ⚠ ***And the caution is the seat's own, stated rather than left to be found***: *a small residual of two large common features is exactly where a nearly-common systematic would sit. **Not an artefact --- it is $7.2$ to $7.7\sigma$ against the ratio's own bands 2--7 scatter, which is much smoother than either spectrum's** --- but **the next reading of the step should be DIFFERENTIAL BY CONSTRUCTION** rather than a difference of two large numbers.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND `r7013`'s TERMINAL CLAUSE IS CORRECTED RATHER THAN DEFENDED.*** *It read that `cc66.57` had already closed the list that existed. **It had not, and the clause rested on the claim this revision withdraws.*** The exits stand as written --- ***OR TERMINATES IF:*** *it is demonstrated that no quantity this construction fixes can produce the step* --- **but the list is open, two channels carry two fifths of it, and nine tenths of the feature is not this cosmology's at all.** ⌗ ***A second exit is a claim like any other and can be wrong; the machinery works by correcting it, not by defending it.*** ⌗ *`cc66.58`, with the pre-registration committed ahead of the working script.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7017` (`cc66.59`) --- THE STEP **SURVIVES** THE DIFFERENTIAL ESTIMATOR, SO THE TENTH IS NOT THE RESIDUAL OF TWO LARGE NUMBERS; **NO SINGLE BILINEAR TERM CARRIES THE SHARED STEP**; AND THE $42$ PER CENT IS $42$ PER CENT OF THE **EXCESS'S** STEP --- THE LARGE READING.*** *Pre-registration as its own commit again, and it **tables first the outcome that ends the row's present object**, which on ⓵ was genuinely on the table.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓵ IT SURVIVES, AND THE ALGEBRA SAYS WHY THE ORDER WAS THE RIGHT ONE.*** *Writing each arm as envelope times oscillation, the present route forms each arm's width and divides --- reading $\mathrm{std}(o_a)/\mathrm{std}(o_c)-1$ --- where the ordered route forms the **ratio of spectra** first and takes one contrast, reading $\mathrm{std}(o_a-o_c)$.* ⇒ ***The common oscillation divides out BEFORE any width is taken: a different statistic and not a cleaner version of the same one.*** *Band-1 departure, four bases, none chosen: **$-0.374$ to $-0.387$ per common $\ell$ at $3.5$--$3.6\sigma$, and $-0.382$ to $-0.383$ per common $q$ at $3.2$--$3.3\sigma$**, against the present route's $-0.566$ to $-0.601$ at $7.2$--$7.7\sigma$.* ⇒ *** SAME SIGN ON ALL FOUR BASES AND BOTH ABSCISSAS, AT ABOUT TWO THIRDS OF THE PRESENT ROUTE'S FRACTIONAL SIZE --- THE TENTH EXISTS IN AN ESTIMATOR IN WHICH THE TWO LARGE NUMBERS ARE NEVER FORMED. *** ⌗ *And the abscissa is a **new** degree of freedom this estimator introduces, the arms differing by $0.085$ per cent in $\ell_A$ --- **so it is not chosen either**, and the two agree to within a fiftieth.* &#9670; ⭭⭭ ***AND A STABILITY CHECK TURNED INTO THE MECHANISM SHOWN DIRECTLY.*** *The conditioning hazard does not fire --- the control never falls below $0.67$ of its band median, so no band divides by a near-zero. **And dividing by a SMOOTHED control instead removes the differencing while keeping the division, and the departure goes POSITIVE, $+0.07$ to $+0.19$, back toward the arms' own $+0.3$.*** ⇒ *** SO IT IS THE DIFFERENCING AND NOT THE DIVISION THAT PRODUCES THE NEGATIVE STEP. *** ⚠ *And the estimator's own limit is pre-registered rather than discovered later: $\mathrm{std}(o_a-o_c)$ responds to a **phase** difference as well as an amplitude one, where the amplitude ratio is blind to phase --- **so a null would have been strong evidence against a differential feature and a signal does not by itself say “amplitude”, and only the STEP may be compared between the two estimators, never the absolute sizes.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓶ A NULL ON THE NAMING, AND THE SEAT CAUGHT ITSELF ABOUT TO MAKE THE UNEARNED INFERENCE.*** *The licence check first, as its own pre-registration required: the 238-multipole decomposition reproduces the 1900-multipole step to better than $0.006$ on **both** arms. Then --- **per-term contrasts showed `dp*dp` with a departure the same size as the total's, and the seat nearly reported it as the carrier. “A matching size is not authorship”**, so it removed each term instead and asked whether the step left with it.* ⇒ *** THE FRACTIONAL LOSSES SUM TO $182$ PER CENT: THE STEP IS NOT ADDITIVE ACROSS THE TERMS, NO SINGLE TERM CARRIES IT, AND IT IS A PROPERTY OF THE SUM. *** ⭭ ***But the leverage is not flat, and that is what the null leaves standing***: *share of the **step** against share of the **oscillation** gives `sw*dp` $5.59\times$, `dp*dp` $3.80\times$, `sw*isw` $1.23\times$, `sw*sw` $0.72\times$ on $88$ per cent of the oscillation, `dp*isw` $-0.91\times$ and `isw*isw` $-2.07\times$.* ⇒ ***A step-weighted reading of the decomposition is led by the Doppler where an amplitude-weighted one is led by the monopole.*** ⚠ *And the exception is named rather than dropped: **`dp*isw` carries a Doppler factor and its leverage is negative, and `isw*isw` sits lower still --- so it is not “every Doppler term leads” and the ordering is not Doppler-versus-not.*** ⛔ *Leverage is **not authorship**, and the $182$ per cent is precisely why.* &#9670; ✔ ***⓷ IT IS THE EXCESS'S STEP --- THE LARGE READING --- ANSWERED FROM `cc66.58`'s OWN FILE AND GATED ON IT RATHER THAN RECALLED.*** *Its share function divides every channel's departure by the departure of $\mathrm{MEAS}-1$, **the excess's own response** --- and that is the only comparison a channel admits, a channel response and the excess both being ratios to the **same control in the same units**.* ⇒ *** SO THE PAIR ACCOUNTS FOR TWO FIFTHS OF THE STEP THE EXCESS ACTUALLY HAS. *** ⌗ ***And the two normalisations are one departure under two denominators, shown rather than asserted***: *band 1's excess is $+0.0215$ where its own trend predicts $+0.0538$, a departure of $-0.0323$ --- which reads as $-0.601$ against the excess's own extrapolated size and $-0.100$ against the control's contrast step, **a factor of six apart on one number** --- and the arms' own log-departures differ by $-0.0308$, **which IS that departure to $4.6$ per cent**.* ⌗ *`cc66.59`, 28 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`; no envelope, no trend basis and no abscissa chosen.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7019` (`cc66.60`) --- THE SURVIVING STEP IS **AMPLITUDE AND NOT PHASE**, SO THE ROW IS **NOT** REFRAMED; **THE CANDIDATE SHARES DO NOT SURVIVE THE CHANGE OF DENOMINATOR** AND ONE CHANNEL REVERSES SIGN; AND **THE STEP DOES NOT COMPOSE THE WAY THE CONTRAST DOES.*** *Pre-registration as its own commit again, tabling **phase** first --- **which was the right way round, because the hazard that fired is the one it named, on the item that would have reframed the row.*** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓵ AMPLITUDE, AND THE SEPARATION IS AN **IDENTITY** RATHER THAN A FIT.*** *For two oscillations of one period, $\mathrm{std}(o_a-o_c) = (A_c/\sqrt2)\sqrt{r^2-2r\cos\Delta+1}$, with the amplitude-only and phase-only parts $(A_c/\sqrt2)|r-1|$ and $(A_c/\sqrt2)\,2|\sin(\Delta/2)|$ --- and **the closed form reproduces the difference's own measured comb amplitude to $0.00$ per cent pointwise**, so the licence gate passes exactly and the split is exact.* *Band-1 departures, four bases, none chosen: **both $-0.204$ to $-0.236$; amplitude only $-0.227$ to $-0.260$; phase only $+0.570$ to $+0.796$ --- the OPPOSITE sign.** At band 1 amplitude supplies $0.958$ of the statistic against phase's $0.284$.* ⇒ *** SO THE FIRST ACOUSTIC CYCLE IS **WEAKENED** IN THIS COSMOLOGY, NOT **DISPLACED**. THE ROW IS NOT REFRAMED AND `P15` KEEPS IT. *** &#9670; ⚠ ***BUT THE PHASE CHANNEL IS UNRESOLVED RATHER THAN MERELY SMALL, AND THE PRE-REGISTERED HAZARD IS THE ONE THAT FIRED.*** *Across window half-widths $0.55$, $0.75$, $0.95$ the phase step runs $+2.93$, $+0.57$, $-0.41$ --- **it changes SIGN --- while the amplitude step does not**, running $-0.105$, $-0.227$, $-0.296$. And the reason is size rather than statistics: **the relative phase is $0.0068$ rad, $0.39$ degrees, against a relative amplitude of $0.0554$.*** ⇒ *** AMPLITUDE ON THE SIGN, INSEPARABLE ON THE SHARE --- the fourth row of the order's own table, taken rather than rounded to the third. *** ⌗ ***And what would separate them, because the order asked for that rather than a shrug***: *the phase read **against the comb itself** over a long lever arm in $q$, whose slope is measurable to a part in $10^3$ over five periods, rather than a local fit in a window one period wide **where amplitude and phase are degenerate by construction**.* &#9670; ⚠⚠ ***AND A CORRECTION TO `cc66.59` THAT THIS DECOMPOSITION FORCED.*** *A band spans $0.70$ in $q$ and the comb period is $1.00$.* ⇒ *** SO A RAW BAND `std` SAMPLES LESS THAN ONE FULL CYCLE AND IS PHASE-DEPENDENT BY CONSTRUCTION, WHERE THE HELD-PERIOD AMPLITUDE IS NOT. *** *Band 1: raw $0.00478$ against held-period$/\sqrt2$ $0.00653$, a ratio of $1.367$. **The step survives on BOTH aggregations** --- $-0.45$ raw and $-0.22$ held-period --- **and neither is chosen**; but `cc66.59` quoted the larger without knowing it was the phase-dependent one, and that is on the record.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓶ AND THE SHARES DO NOT SURVIVE --- THE ROW THE ORDER TABLED, AND THE STRONGEST OF THE THREE.*** *Each knob read where the CR-specific tenth actually lives, by its **own** $\mathrm{std}(o_{\rm knob}-o_{\rm lcdm})$ against the arm's: **the window weighting is $+1.05$ to $+1.32$, a step of the OPPOSITE sign to the arm**, where on the ratio-of-contrasts route it merely had the wrong sign; **the term mix is $-0.11$ to $-0.21$ and is no longer a step at all**, where it carried $63$ per cent; and **the JOINT is $-0.07$ to $+0.03$, changing sign across the bases and measuring zero within its own scatter**, where it carried $42$ per cent.* ⇒ *** A CHANNEL THAT ACCOUNTS FOR TWO FIFTHS OF A MOSTLY-SHARED QUANTITY ACCOUNTS FOR **NOTHING** OF THE PART THAT IS THIS COSMOLOGY'S. THE ROW'S CANDIDATE ACCOUNTING WAS SCORED AGAINST THE WRONG OBJECT. *** ⚠ ***And the seat takes the weaker word***: *the term mix and the pair are **not resolved** on this estimator and are **not excluded** --- which is what the scatter supports and is a different claim.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓷ AND THE STEP DOES NOT COMPOSE THE WAY THE CONTRAST DOES.*** *`cc66.49` measured the **contrast** composing multiplicatively, the product in 7 of 7 bands and the sum in 0 of 7. On the **step**, from the two singles' departures $+1.184$ and $-0.156$: **product predicts $+0.843$, sum $+1.028$, quadrature $+1.195$ --- against a measured joint of $-0.014$**, at $6.1$, $7.4$ and $8.6$ times the joint's own scatter.* ⇒ *** NO RULE FITS: ALL THREE PREDICT A LARGE **POSITIVE** DEPARTURE WHERE THE REALISED PAIR MEASURES ZERO. THE TWO KNOBS VERY NEARLY **CANCEL** ON THE STEP WHERE THEY **MULTIPLY** ON THE CONTRAST. *** *A property of the step and not of the knobs.* ⌗ ***And the residuals are in departure units against that scatter rather than as a percentage***, *the joint's departure being consistent with zero and a percentage of a near-zero measurement being no share at all --- **this line's own denominator guard turned on itself**.* ⌗ *`cc66.60`, 23 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`; no envelope, basis, abscissa **or aggregation** chosen, and no mechanism proposed --- “the first acoustic cycle is weaker in this cosmology” being a signature and not a cause.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7021` (`cc66.61`) --- **NO STATISTIC THIS CONSTRUCTION CAN BUILD RESOLVES THE TWO CHANNELS**, WHICH IS THIS ROW'S TERMINAL CONDITION ARRIVING **WITH A NUMBER**; THE PHASE STEP **DIES ON A MATCHED-WIDTH CONTROL** SO THE AMPLITUDE VERDICT IS COMPLETE; AND THE CANCELLATION IS **AGGREGATION-DEPENDENT**.*** *Pre-registration as its own commit, defining the resolving ratio before computing it, naming four routes to a smaller scatter and committing to test each, **naming in advance the one it expected most from**, and naming the abuse hazard before meeting it. **Both of those last two mattered: the named favourite FAILED and the hazard FIRED.*** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓵ THE FLOOR DOES NOT MOVE.*** *A channel is decided when its band-1 departure exceeds twice its own bands 2--7 scatter, so the **minimum resolvable share** is $f_{\min} = 2\sigma/|d_{\rm arm}|$, defined before it was computed. Across every route banked --- both aggregations, both abscissas, seven bands, eight bands out to $q = 6.45$, twelve finer bands --- $f_{\min}$ runs $0.75$, $0.65$, $0.65$, $0.63$, $0.63$, $0.62$ and at best **$0.59$**.* ⇒ *** THE TERM MIX CARRIES $0.35$ OF THE STEP AND THE REALISED PAIR $0.03$, AGAINST A FLOOR OF $0.59$. THEY CANNOT BE RESOLVED BY ANY STATISTIC THIS CONSTRUCTION CAN BUILD. *** &#9670; ⚠ ***AND THE REASON IS THE ONE THE SEAT NAMED IN ADVANCE AND GOT WRONG, WHICH IS WHY NAMING IT IN ADVANCE WAS WORTH IT.*** *It wrote that the held-period aggregation was the candidate it expected most from, **before** measuring.* ⇒ ***It lowers $\sigma$ by $2.12\times$ --- exactly the predicted reduction --- AND LOWERS THE SIGNAL BY $2.06\times$ AT THE SAME TIME***, *moving $f_{\min}$ from $0.65$ to $0.63$.* ⌗ *** A REDUCTION IN SCATTER IS ONLY A GAIN IF THE SIGNAL DOES NOT FALL WITH IT. *** *The mechanism predicted and the consequence not.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE ABUSE HAZARD FIRED BEFORE ANY POWER CLAIM COULD BE MADE, AND IT IS WORSE THAN THE HAZARD.*** *Raw against held-period: the window channel holds $+1.1843$ on both, **but the term mix goes $-0.1564 \to +0.0781$ and the JOINT $-0.0139 \to +0.1824$ --- BOTH SIGNS FLIP.*** ⇒ *** SO MORE POWER WOULD NOT DECIDE THEM: THEIR DEPARTURES ARE AGGREGATION-DEPENDENT AND NOT ESTABLISHED AT ALL. *** *Only the channel that steps the wrong way keeps its sign on all eight readings.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***⓶ THE PHASE IS RESOLVED WHERE IT IS NOT NEEDED AND NOT WHERE IT IS --- AND THE SEAT NEARLY REPORTED THE OPPOSITE.*** *The order's own prescription executed: over four disjoint **full-period** stretches $\Delta = +0.007368 \pm 0.000974$ rad, $+0.42$ degrees, **a $7.6\sigma$ determination**, agreeing with the whole upper range read as one stretch of $4.2$ periods. Band 1 gives $-0.002440$ rad, **$5.03$ scatters out on that yardstick --- and for about a minute that looked like the order's second row, the estimator not measuring what `cc66.60` assumed.*** ⛔ ***BUT THAT YARDSTICK IS WRONG:*** *band 1 is $0.70$ of a period, and **a projection over a non-integer number of periods leaks the baseline**, so a narrow stretch is both noisier **and** biased --- the only licensed comparison being against stretches of the same width, whose scatter is $0.007264$ rad, **$3.7\times$ the full-period one**.* ⇒ *** BAND 1 SITS $1.52$ SCATTERS OUT, NOT $5.03$. THE PHASE STEP IS NOT RESOLVED AND `cc66.60`'s AMPLITUDE VERDICT STANDS AS THE COMPLETE ONE RATHER THAN THE SIGN-ONLY ONE. *** ⌗ ***And the limit is now met in three places***: *the method reaches a $13$ per cent measurement over five periods and cannot bring it to the one band that needs it. **Band 1 is narrower than every period this construction measures with**, met on the window-free family at `cc66.58`, on the amplitude/phase split at `cc66.60`, and here --- **three revisions' evidence that it is a property of the band and not of the method.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***⓷ AND THE CANCELLATION RETURNS NO WORD, WHICH IS THE THIRD ROW THE PRE-REGISTRATION TABLED.*** *The joint's departure over its own scatter: **raw $0.24$ to $0.54$, consistent with zero on all four bases; held-period $4.16$ to $4.98$, on none**.* ⇒ *** NEITHER EXACT NOR APPROXIMATE --- IT IS AGGREGATION-DEPENDENT, SO ⓷ INHERITS ⓵'s ANSWER RATHER THAN CHOOSING A WORD. *** *The constraint-versus-coincidence question cannot be settled here.* ⌗ *And what it would be cancelling between is large either way, the two singles summing to $+0.895$ raw and $+1.149$ held --- **not two small numbers agreeing to vanish**.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE TERMINAL CLAUSE IS AMENDED TO THE SENTENCE THAT IS EARNED, ON THE DELIVERING SEAT'S OWN INSISTENCE RATHER THAN THIS SEAT'S.*** *Its words: **the honest form is narrower than “no quantity this construction fixes produces the step” --- what is demonstrated is that no statistic this construction can build could tell whether they do. Those are different sentences and only the second is earned.*** ⇒ ***OR TERMINATES IF:*** *it is demonstrated that **no statistic this construction can build could tell whether any quantity it fixes produces the step** --- proved over the instrument's whole reach rather than inferred from failures.* ⌗ ***That clause is now very nearly met***: *the floor is measured over every banked route, the two candidate departures do not hold their sign across aggregations, and the band-width limit is met in three independent places. **What is not yet covered is an instrument that does not band at all**, which is the order.* ⌗ *`cc66.61`, 22 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`; no envelope, basis, abscissa or aggregation chosen.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7023` (`cc66.62`) --- **THE LIKELIHOOD SEES THE STEP AND SEPARATES THE TWO CHANNELS AT TWENTY-FIVE SIGMA.** SO `cc66.61`'s DEMONSTRATION COVERS THE **STATISTIC FAMILY** AND NOT THE INSTRUMENT THE PAPER RUNS BESIDE IT, AND **THE AMENDED CLAUSE IS NOT MET. THE ROW REOPENS WITH A MEASUREMENT TO MAKE RATHER THAN AN EXIT TO TAKE.*** ⌗ ***And the seat says the thing this seat needed to hear***: *“you asked rather than struck, and that was right --- the exit would have been taken on a demonstration with a live instrument outside it.”* &#9670; ⭭⭭ ***⓵ⓐ IT BINS, IT DOES NOT **BAND**, AND THE MARGIN IS NOT CLOSE.*** *The likelihood's median bin width is $0.0298$ in $q$ --- **one thirty-fourth of a comb period**, against the banded family's seven tenths of one --- with **24 bins inside band 1**, and a bin-to-bin correlation that is a flat $\approx0.15$ floor across lags 1--7 rather than a coupling growing over a period.* ⇒ ***So it does not inherit the width limit through its covariance either, which was the other way the order allowed the answer to arrive.*** &#9670; ⭭⭭ ***⓵ⓑ AND THE STEP IS A LOCALISED CONTRIBUTION TO ITS EXCESS.*** *Arm against control from each band's bins alone: **$36.4$, $42.8$, $49.8$, $44.6$, $35.1$, $24.6$, $14.2\sigma$**, with the whole covered range at $98.9\sigma$. Band 1 against its own bands 2--7 trend is $-0.311$ to $-0.489$ on the four bases at $2.5$--$3.2\sigma$ of its own residual --- **the trend predicts $53$--$71\sigma$ at band 1 and the likelihood delivers $36.4$**.* ⇒ ***The same sign as every other instrument in this row --- and the first per-band number in it whose uncertainty is THE INSTRUMENT'S OWN NOISE rather than an empirical scatter across a noiseless theory spectrum.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓵ⓒ AND IT SEPARATES THE TWO CHANNELS AT BAND 1 --- THE THING NO STATISTIC IN THIS ROW CAN DO.*** *At band 1: window against control $6.61\sigma$, term mix against control $18.64$, the joint against control $12.82$, the arm against control $36.37$ --- and* *** WINDOW AGAINST TERM MIX AT $25.11\sigma$, against the $2\sigma$ the pre-registration set as the bar. *** ⇒ *** SO THE AMENDED CLAUSE IS NOT MET AND THE ROW HAS A MEASUREMENT TO MAKE. *** &#9670; ⚠ ***AND THE TWO FLOORS ARE NOT ONE NUMBER, WRITTEN DOWN BEFORE EITHER WAS IN HAND.*** *The likelihood's smallest detectable share at band 1 is $0.055$ --- of the band-1 **difference**, under **real instrument noise**. `cc66.61`'s floor is $0.59$ --- of the **step**, under an **empirical scatter of a noiseless spectrum**.* ⛔ ***Shares of different quantities under different kinds of uncertainty: neither bounds the other and a ratio of them means nothing.*** *The legitimate comparison needs neither figure:* **the statistic family cannot separate the channels at band 1; the likelihood separates them at twenty-five sigma.** &#9670; ✔ ***⓶ THE SCOPE STATEMENT, COMPLETE RATHER THAN REPRESENTATIVE --- ELEVEN INSTRUMENTS, AND **EIGHT OF THE ELEVEN BAND**.*** *The contrast statistic, the held-period amplitude, the window-free peak-to-trough depth, the extremal envelope, the differential estimator, the bilinear decomposition read through the band statistic, the comb-phase projection (**bands by another name**) and the projection-width kernel all average over $0.70$ of a period. **Three do not**: the anchored peak/trough locator, the likelihood, and the refit that uses the likelihood's bins.* ⇒ ***And of those three the anchored locator makes no amplitude claim at band 1 --- it locates peaks*** ⇒ *** SO THE LIKELIHOOD AND ITS REFIT ARE THE ONLY INSTRUMENTS IN THIS ROW THAT BOTH AVOID THE BAND-WIDTH LIMIT AND MAKE AN AMPLITUDE CLAIM AT BAND 1 --- AND THERE IS NO THIRD INSTRUMENT OUTSIDE THE DEMONSTRATION, WHICH IS THE THING THIS SEAT SAID IT WOULD NOT STRIKE WITHOUT. *** &#9670; ⭭ ***AND THE PRE-REGISTRATION DID SOMETHING NEW, FLAGGED RATHER THAN BURIED:*** *it opens by saying **which facts were already in hand when it was written** --- the likelihood's bin structure, inspected first because it is a property of the instrument fixed on disk regardless of any spectrum. **So the order's first branch was already settled and was declared settled rather than tabled as open.*** ⌗ *In the seat's words: **pre-registering a question I had already answered would have been theatre.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***SO THE TERMINAL CLAUSE RETURNS TO ITS STRONG FORM, BECAUSE WHAT MADE THE WEAK FORM NECESSARY WAS AN INSTRUMENT LIMITATION AND NOT THE CONSTRUCTION'S.*** ***OR TERMINATES IF:*** *it is demonstrated that **no quantity this construction fixes produces the step**, measured in the likelihood's own metric --- where the separation exists and the banded family's floor does not apply.* ⌗ ***And the weak form is kept as what it is***: *a proved statement about the banded family, landed in `P15` as that and not as a statement about the comparison.* ⌗ *`cc66.62`, 19 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`, with `cc66.61`'s floor neither revisited nor softened, as ordered.* &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***`r7025` (`cc66.63`) --- **BAND 1 CARRIES UNDER ONE PER CENT OF THE LIKELIHOOD'S EXCESS AND CHANGES SIGN UNDER SHAPE ABSORPTION**, WHICH IS NEITHER OF THE TWO OUTCOMES THE ORDER TABLED; THAT FORCES A CORRECTION TO `cc66.62`'s LABEL; AND IN THE METRIC THE STEP DOES LIVE IN, **ALL THREE CHANNELS DEPART THE ARM'S WAY**.*** *And the pre-registration takes the order's separating-power-against-significance distinction as **the file's vocabulary** rather than acknowledging it --- a table fixing which term means which number, and a commitment that **no quantity divides a separating power by a significance** --- with ⓶ stated as asked FIRST though numbered second, **so the file's structure carries the point instead of a sentence.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***⓶ FIRST, AND THE ANSWER IS A THIRD THING.*** *Across one to four free coefficients in the log of the multipole, band 1's contribution to the excess runs $+2.6$, $-0.5$, $+0.3$, $+6.6$ --- **$0.9$ per cent of the $287$ the seven bands sum to, and it CHANGES SIGN** --- where bands 2--7 hold to within a few per cent.* ⇒ *** SO BAND 1'S DEPARTURE IS NOT A DISTINCT FEATURE OF THE RESIDUAL AND NOT THE SHAPE REJECTION READ LOCALLY EITHER, BECAUSE THE SHAPE REJECTION IS BARELY PRESENT THERE AT ALL: IT LIVES AT BANDS 4--7, WHICH CARRY $86$ PER CENT OF THE PER-BAND EXCESS. *** ⌗ *The order assumed the departure was one of those two. **It is a third: the likelihood rejects this arm decisively and does so somewhere else entirely.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THAT FORCES A CORRECTION TO `cc66.62`, WHICH THE SEAT OWNS IN TERMS AND WHICH IS THE CLASS THE ORDER HAD JUST NAMED.*** *`cc66.62` ⓵ⓑ was headed “THE STEP IS A LOCALISED CONTRIBUTION TO ITS EXCESS” and then reported band 1's **separating power**.* ⇒ *** THE NUMBER WAS RIGHT AND THE LABEL WAS WRONG: THE LIKELIHOOD DOES SEE A BAND-1 DEPARTURE IN ITS POWER TO TELL MODELS APART, AND IT DOES NOT CARRY A BAND-1 EXCESS. *** ⌗ ***In the seat's words:*** *“those are different sentences and I ran them together --- one revision after you drew the distinction, and one before it bit. You put it in front of the order; it belonged in front of my last one.”* ⌗ *And `P15` is corrected to match: what this seat landed at `r7023` carried the same wrong label.* &#9670; ⭭⭭ ***⓵ AND IN THE METRIC THE STEP ACTUALLY LIVES IN, ALL THREE CHANNELS DEPART THE ARM'S WAY --- THE FIRST TIME THIS ROW HAS HAD THAT.*** *Each channel's band-1 departure from its own bands 2--7 trend, in the likelihood's units, against the arm's: **window $1.31$ of the arm's at $2.7$--$3.6\sigma$, term mix $0.86$ at $2.2$--$2.4$, the JOINT $0.51$ at $0.6$--$1.9$**, against the arm's own $2.5$--$3.2$.* ⚠ ***And the window channel REVERSES ITS VERDICT BETWEEN INSTRUMENTS***: *it went the **wrong** way on the differential estimator and goes the right way here and **over-delivers**. **Reported as a difference between instruments rather than smoothed into a story.*** ⌗ *And the cancellation is **measured** rather than read off the two significances the order flagged: product $-0.677$, sum $-0.851$, quadrature $-0.613$ against a measured $-0.208$ --- **no rule fits and the realised pair delivers $34$ per cent of the closest**. ⇒ *The two knobs partly cancelling is now seen on **two** instruments, and is beginning to look like a property rather than a coincidence.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND WHAT ⓶ DOES TO ⓵ IS CARRIED IN THE SAME BREATH, WHICH IS WHY IT WAS RUN FIRST.*** *Those shares are shares of a **separating-power** departure, and ⓶ says that feature carries one per cent of the likelihood's excess.* ⇒ *** SO THIS IS NOT YET “A CANDIDATE PRODUCES THE STEP” IN THE SENSE THE STRONG CLAUSE NEEDS. IT IS A REAL MATCH, IN A REAL METRIC, AND IT IS THE WRONG METRIC FOR THE CLAUSE. *** &#9670; ⭭⭭⭭ ***AND THE GATING SEAT TAKES THE FORK RATHER THAN LEAVING IT: THE CLAUSE FOLLOWS THE COST AND NOT THE STRUCTURE.*** *The seat offered two readings of “the step” and said the choice was this seat's. **Neither, taken alone.** `P15` already records that the contrast excess carries three quarters of the likelihood's excess; `cc66.63` now locates that cost at **bands 4--7**, where `cc66.58` had already measured the excess to be **featureless, a constant fitting it**.* ⇒ *** SO THE ROW'S OWN FINDING, ONE LEVEL UP FROM `cc66.56`'s: THE COMPARISON'S DISCRIMINATING STRUCTURE AND THE DATA'S REJECTION LIVE IN DIFFERENT BANDS. THE STEP IS THE EXCESS'S MOST PROMINENT FEATURE AND VERY NEARLY ITS CHEAPEST. *** ⇒ ***OR TERMINATES IF:*** *it is demonstrated that no quantity this construction fixes produces the contrast excess **in the bands where the likelihood rejects the arm**.* **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *the carrier identified there, in the likelihood's own metric.* ⌗ ***And the step is not discarded by that move***: *it is a characterised feature --- amplitude not phase, nine tenths shared, at the second acoustic peak, with three candidates matching it in separating power --- **which is now known not to be where the arm is rejected**.* ⌗ *`cc66.63`, 21 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`; no new candidate, no mechanism, and `cc66.61`'s floor left as finished work.* &#9670; ⚭⚭⚭ ***`cc66.64` (`r7025` FILLED, LANDED `r7029`) --- THE REJECTION AT BANDS 4--7 IS **FEATURELESS TO EVERY SMOOTH SHAPE AND MODULATED AT THE ACOUSTIC PERIOD AT ONCE**, SO `cc66.58`'s "FEATURELESS" WAS A PROPERTY OF THE **BANDING**; THE **TERM MIX** IS THE FIRST CHANNEL IN THIS ROW'S HISTORY TO COST SOMETHING WHERE THE ARM IS ACTUALLY REJECTED; AND **THE CANCELLATION IS ABSENT --- THE PAIR ADDS**.*** &#9670; ⛭ ***THE DELIVERING SEAT CORRECTED ITS OWN INSTRUMENT BEFORE USING IT AGAIN, AND THE CORRECTION IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING.*** *`cc66.63`'s per-band numbers inverted each band's covariance block alone and summed to 287 against 322 --- a gap it quoted rather than elided. Since the arm's residual is the control's less the difference of the spectra, the excess is **identically** a term in that difference alone plus a term coupling it to the control's residual, whose per-bin parts sum to the total to $1.7\times10^{-13}$.* ⇒ *** AND THAT SPLIT IS THE SEPARATING-POWER/EXCESS DISTINCTION WRITTEN AS ALGEBRA: THE FIRST TERM NEVER ASKS WHERE THE DATA SITS AND THE SECOND IS THE ONLY PART THAT DOES. *** ⌸ *Which is why a quantity can stand at $25\sigma$ in one and at nothing in the other --- `cc66.63`'s whole finding, now an identity rather than two measurements that happened to disagree.* *On the exact decomposition bands 4--7 carry **87\%** against the approximation's 86\%: **the location holds and the instrument is what was corrected**.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***THE RESULT. THE REJECTION IS NOT A LEVEL.*** *Across the 82 bins those four bands hold, a constant explains **0.0\%** of the scatter, a trend **1.6\%** and a quadratic **2.8\%** --- `cc66.58` intact at the finer scale --- **yet the projection at the comb's own period returns 7.30 against a null of 2.53 built from 110 wrong periods, NOT ONE of which returns more, with the scan's own maximum at period 1.01**.* ⇒ *** SO SEVEN NUMBERS EACH AVERAGING 0.70 OF A PERIOD CANNOT CARRY A MODULATION AT THAT PERIOD, AND `cc66.58`'s FEATURELESSNESS WAS THE BANDING'S PROPERTY AND NOT THE SPECTRUM'S. *** ⌸ ***It is the band-width limit a fourth time, at the other end of the range*** --- *`cc66.58` met it on the window-free family, `cc66.60` on the amplitude/phase trade-off, `cc66.61` on the phase, and here on a modulation it hid rather than a share it could not resolve.* ⛔ ***And the obvious artefact was killed before the result was reported***: *the difference of the spectra is itself a comb, so a comb in the excess could be the difference looking at itself. Split by term: **6.24 from the data-coupling term against 1.08 from the difference-alone term** ⇒ five sixths of the modulation comes from the term that knows where the data sits. **It is a fact about the data.** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND IN THAT METRIC ONE CANDIDATE CHANNEL COSTS THE ARM'S WAY, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME THIS ROW HAS HELD ONE.*** *Shares at bands 4--7, stable across $n=1..4$ free shape coefficients: window **0.14**, term mix **1.36**, joint **1.53**.* ⛔ ***A share above one is not a result on its own*** --- *any spectrum that is not the control costs something, so 1.36 may mean only "also rejected" and not "rejected the same way". **So the per-bin PATTERN was compared, and it is the pattern that decides.*** &#9670; ⚠ ***The window weighting is ANTI-correlated with the arm, $r=-0.70$*** --- *its 0.14 is **a different cost and not a small share of the same one**, and last revision it was the strongest match in separating power at 1.31. Reverse standing on the two metrics.* &#9670; ⛭ ***The term mix matches at $r=+0.70$, regression slope 0.74, 45\% left over***, *1.41/1.07/0.76 across bands 4, 5 and 7* ⇒ ***the first channel this row has produced that costs something where the likelihood actually rejects the arm.*** &#9670; ⛔ ***The JOINT matches WORSE than the term mix alone, $r=+0.46$*** --- *adding the window raises the total and degrades the pattern, which is the sharpest form in which this row can state that **a total is not a match**.* &#9670; ⚠⚠ ***AND THE ONE THAT COSTS THE ACCOUNT: THE CANCELLATION IS GONE.*** *This seat had written that it was beginning to look like a property of the pair. Measured here: **singles sum to 1.4965 and the realised pair gives 1.5305 --- 102\% of the sum. THE KNOBS ADD.*** ⇒ *** CANCELLATION IS A PROPERTY OF THE METRIC AND NOT OF THE PAIR. *** ⌸ *Third instrument-dependence this row has found and the only one running against something that was being built --- **two instruments agreeing twice was not enough, and this seat should not have let it start to sound like one**.* &#9670; ⌸ ***TWO DISCIPLINE ITEMS THE DELIVERING SEAT REPORTED AGAINST ITSELF.*** *A pre-registered prediction **failed and is reported rather than dropped**: the quadratic term was predicted to enter at period 1/2 and carries 0.57 there against 1.08 at 1.00, the difference of the spectra being no pure sinusoid. And **band 6 is flagged rather than quoted** --- it is where the arm is cheapest (14.6 against 123 at band 7), so every channel's share there has a small denominator and none is quoted alone.* ⌸ *`P15_the_term_mix_is_the_first_channel_to_produce_cost_where_the_arm_is_rejected_and_the_excess_there_is_combed_at_the_acoustic_period`, 24 gates, `GATES: ALL PASS`; pre-registration its own commit ahead of the working script; no new candidate, no mechanism, `cc66.61`'s floor and the band-1 results left as finished work.* &#9670; ⛭ ***THIS SEAT'S ADJUDICATION, AND IT IS WHY THE CLAUSE DOES NOT MOVE.*** *The two findings point the same way --- the rejection carries acoustic structure, and the term mix is the only channel whose cost is in the right places --- and **the delivering seat deliberately did not write the sentence joining them, because joining them is a mechanism and its own guard forbids it. That restraint is correct.** ⇒ *** AND WHAT JOINS THEM IS A MEASUREMENT AND NOT A SENTENCE: WHETHER THE TERM MIX'S OWN COST IS COMBED AT THE SAME PERIOD, AND WHETHER THE 45\% IT MISPLACES IS THE PART THAT IS NOT. *** ⌸ *That is ordered at `r7029` and it is the first measurement in this row's history that could **close** the clause rather than narrow it: the correlation is measured on per-band shares and the modulation on per-bin structure, so the two results are not yet the same statement about the same object.* &#9670; ⚭⚭⚭ ***`cc66.65` (`r7029` FILLED) AND `r7029+70.1`/`+70.2` (THE SECOND SEAT'S FIRST TWO DELIVERIES IN THIS LANE) --- **THE TERM MIX'S OWN COST IS NOT COMBED AND THE MODULATION SURVIVES IN THE PART IT DOES NOT EXPLAIN, WHICH IS THE TERMINATING ROW; THE NULL THAT SCORES IT IS WORTH ONE IN FOUR AND NOT ONE IN A HUNDRED AND ELEVEN; AND THE BANDED INSTRUMENTS DO NOT MERELY ATTENUATE A COMB-PERIOD FEATURE, THEY CANNOT REPRESENT ONE.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***THE DISCIPLINE THAT GAVE THE REVISION AN ANSWER AT ALL, AND IT WAS WRITTEN DOWN BEFORE THE RUN.*** *A one-coefficient regression's fitted part is a scalar multiple of one of its inputs **in either orientation**, so it carries nothing its input did not. Measured: orientation A returns $\beta\times7.304=5.4304$ and orientation B $\gamma\times4.238=3.4044$, **both exact to the digit**.* ⇒ *** SO THE ORDER'S THREE NUMBERS COLLAPSE TO ONE QUESTION --- IS THE CHANNEL'S OWN COST COMBED --- AND THE ONE READING THAT WOULD HAVE SAID *DISCHARGES* RESTS ENTIRELY ON AN AMPLITUDE THAT IS ARITHMETIC. *** ⌗ *Both orientations were run rather than one picked, because picking one silently would have decided this row on a scalar multiple.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***(1) THE ANSWER IS NO, AND THE MODULATION IS IN WHAT NO CHANNEL ACCOUNTS FOR.*** *Term mix's own cost **4.238** against a null maximum of 4.297, **1** of 110 above. The 45 per cent it misplaces **1.482** against 3.188, **all 110** above --- decisively not combed. **What it does NOT explain: 4.005 against 3.889, NONE of the 110 above, at $-0.16$ rad from the arm's own phase --- it clears.*** ⇒ *** THAT IS THE TERMINATING ROW, DELIVERED WITHOUT BEING SOFTENED INTO A SIXTH NARROWING. *** ⌗ *And the self-similarity artefact was killed again for the channel as ordered: the term mix clears its null in **neither** term ($d^{T}Fd$ 0.851 against 0.907; cross 3.399 against 3.728), so there is no channel-looking-at-itself to discount.* &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***(2) THE WINDOW IS COMBED IN ANTIPHASE ON THE COST --- AND IN PHASE ON THE SPECTRA, WHICH LOCATES THE REVERSAL WITHOUT NAMING IT.*** *Its cost projects at **2.010** against a null maximum of 1.711, none above, at $+3.12$ rad from the arm's --- $\pi$ to within 0.02, **so it is a structure and not a smooth offset**.* ⛔⛔ *But "one structure read with two signs" does **not** survive its own test: $\alpha=-0.059$, the residual keeping **98 per cent** of the window's power and the amplitudes missing by a factor of five.* ⌗ ***And the delivering seat named that blunt test as the wrong instrument*** --- *a ratio over whole vectors asks whether the window **is** the arm scaled, where the question is about each one's **modulated part**, which is `cc66.60`'s aggregation error met in a new place and named rather than repeated.* ⇒ *Like-for-like on the two spectrum differences: the window's modulation is **$+0.27$ rad from the arm's --- IN PHASE** --- at a ratio of 0.264, and $0.264\times6.241=1.646$ against a measured 1.893.* ⇒ *** THE MAGNITUDE COMPOSES AND THE SIGN DOES NOT: THE REVERSAL APPEARS ONLY AFTER THE LIKELIHOOD'S OWN WEIGHTING. *** ⌗ *Located, not explained; naming why would be a mechanism.* &#9670; ✔✔ ***AND THE SECOND SEAT'S AUDIT OF THE NULL LANDS ON EXACTLY THIS RESULT, WHICH IS WHY IT WAS PUT THERE.*** *`cc66.64`'s comb is **confirmed and strengthened**, by a seat that did not build it: against **2,000 draws of the instrument's own covariance** carried through the receipt's own pipeline, 7.30 clears a null maximum of **2.54** with **0** reaching it ($p\le0.0005$); against **45 valid circular shifts**, 0 reaching it. The five-sixths attribution survives both --- the cross term 6.24 against a 99th percentile of 1.08, 0 of 2,000 --- and the quadratic term has **no noise in it to fail**, being 1.055 $\pm$ 0.004 under the noise null, which is exactly what `cc66.64` said it was.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***BUT THE WRONG-PERIOD NULL IS LOOSER THAN IT READS, AND THAT BEARS DIRECTLY ON `cc66.65`'s MARGIN.*** *The 82 bins span $T=2.78$ in $q$, so the range holds about **3.6 independent frequencies** and the 110-period null has an effective count of **$N_{\rm eff}=3.07$** by the participation ratio of its own correlation matrix.* ⇒ *** "NOT ONE OF 110" IS NOT ONE OF ABOUT THREE: AS A PROBABILITY IT IS WORTH ROUGHLY ONE IN FOUR, NOT ONE IN 111 --- AND A FINER GRID CANNOT FIX IT, THE GRID ALREADY BEING THIRTY TIMES FINER THAN THE RESOLUTION. *** ⌗ *And the period is localised to a **peak spanning 0.88--1.19**, not to the grid step at 1.01: the acoustic period to within $\pm0.15$.* ✔ *No aliasing found: the bin Nyquist period is about 0.06, far from the scan; and band-level structure does leak (a unit alternating per-band pattern projects 0.375 at period 1.00) but carries only 1.26 of the 7.30 against the within-band 6.16 --- **the comb is within the bands, not an alias of them**.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND THE RESOLUTION TABLE REDUCES TO ONE LINE THAT IS SHARPER THAN THE LIMIT IT REPLACES.*** *A banded instrument outputs one number per 0.70-wide band; **seven numbers at 0.70 spacing have a Nyquist period of 1.40**, so a feature at the comb period is **not representable** by them --- not attenuated, unrepresentable --- and the band's own boxcar passes only **0.37** of such a modulation before that. The likelihood bins at 0.0298: a Nyquist period of **0.060** and a transfer of **0.9985** at the comb.* ⇒ *** SO THE FOUR ACCIDENTAL DISCOVERIES OF THIS LIMIT --- `cc66.58`, `cc66.60`, `cc66.61`, `cc66.64` --- WERE FOUR RESULTS STATED ON INSTRUMENTS WHOSE FINEST PERIOD IS 1.40 OR COARSER, ABOUT A FEATURE AT PERIOD 1.00. THE TABLE'S COLUMN WOULD HAVE SAID SO FIRST. *** ⌗ *Eight of the eleven sit at 1.40 or coarser; the likelihood and its refit at 0.060. And **four smallest-share cells are honest blanks** with the reason in each: three instruments carry no noise model at all, and the refit's inputs are not in the repository --- a guessed floor in any of them would have been worse than the blank.* ⌗ *One claim of that seat's was narrowed by its own gate rather than the gate loosened: "no covariance anywhere" was false, the contrast receipt reading the covariance once as the weight of an amplitude fit, so the claim became "a fitting weight, not a noise model: nothing drawn from it and nothing propagated", gated twice.* &#9670; ✔ ***ONE FINDING AGAINST A LANDED CLAIM OF `cc66.62`'s, ROUTED RATHER THAN CORRECTED HERE.*** *`cc66.62` says the anchored locator "makes no amplitude claim at band 1", and that holds **only** in the sense that it quotes no band-1 number: **its first trough anchor is inside band 1**, averaged with two outside. `P15` does not carry the instrument-scope claim, so no paper text is affected; routed to the owning seat at `r7033`.* &#9670; ⌗ *`P15_the_term_mix_does_not_carry_the_modulation_and_it_survives_in_the_part_no_channel_explains`, 20 gates; `P15_the_resolution_table_eight_of_the_eleven_instruments_cannot_represent_the_comb_period_and_four_smallest_shares_are_honest_blanks`, 24 gates; and the null audit's pre-registration, script and output under `computations/beyond_the_wall/r7029_70_null_audit/`. All `GATES: ALL PASS`; each pre-registration its own commit ahead of its script. Landed in `P15` `sec:refit-bound` and `sec:scope` at `r7033`.* &#9670; ⚭⚭⚭ ***`cc66.66` (`r7033` FILLED) AND `r7033+70.1` --- **ALL THREE PROJECTIONS CLEAR THE VALIDATED NULL, SO THE CHANNEL'S COST IS COMBED AFTER ALL AND `cc66.65`'s CENTRAL READING WAS WRONG; THE FORK THE GATE POSED DOES NOT DISCRIMINATE AND THAT IS THE GATE'S DEFECT; AND TEN PASSAGES OF `P15` QUOTED A SIGNIFICANCE OR SHARE THEIR INSTRUMENT CANNOT BEAR, ALL TEN NOW CORRECTED.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***THE DELIVERING SEAT PRE-REGISTERED THE WORDS FOR ITS OWN REVERSAL BEFORE RUNNING ANYTHING.*** *Its `PREDICTION.md`: "a bar worth one in four failing something by one unit is equally capable of passing it. If the channel's own cost clears the noise null, `cc66.65`'s reading was wrong in the direction I did not flag, and I will say that in those words."* ⇒ *** IT CLEARED, AND THOSE WERE THE WORDS. *** ⌗ *And it named which part of `cc66.65` was weak: **not** the arithmetic gate this seat had promoted, which stands --- **the bar, and the bar was its own**. It flagged the margin as thin and asked for the audit; it did not work out that 82 bins over $T=2.78$ hold about 3.6 independent frequencies, which is the fact that decides it.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***THE NUMBERS, ON 70's INSTRUMENT RATHER THAN ON A RE-DERIVATION OF IT*** *(the delivering seat copied `shape_fit`, `detrend`, `amp` and `terms` rather than rebuilding them, and reproduced the arm first: 7.3038, 0 of 2,000, null max 2.34 against 70's 2.54 on another seed).* &#9670; *Term mix's own cost **4.238** against a null max of **1.874**, 0 of 2,000 --- **2.26x**. The part of the arm's excess it does NOT explain **4.005** against **1.416** --- **2.83x**. The 45 per cent it misplaces **1.482** against **1.029** --- **1.44x**, which the delivering seat declined to round into the same sentence as the other two.* &#9670; ✔ ***AND THE CLEARANCE IS NOT THE NOISE-FREE TERM TALKING***, *which is the check that makes it a result rather than an artefact of the null's construction: under this null the spectra's difference is held fixed and only the control's residual moves, so the quadratic term **cannot fail** --- measured at 0.851 against $0.794 \pm 0.006$ and **reported as no evidence** --- while the cross term is 3.399 against a null median of 0.354, 0 of 2,000.* ⇒ ***The channel clears on the term that knows where the data sits.*** ⌗ *The held-coefficient variant agrees on both fitted quantities, so none of the clearance is the regression chasing noise, which the pre-registration required before any exit.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND THE OLD BAR WAS NOT MERELY WEAK, IT WAS INFLATED: its maxima ran 3.2--4.3 where this one's run 1.0--1.9.*** ⇒ *** SO THE WRONG-PERIOD AMPLITUDES WERE CARRYING THE SIGNAL ITSELF, LEAKED --- AND THE GENERAL FORM IS WORTH STATING AS ONE: A NULL BUILT FROM THE SAME DATA AT NEIGHBOURING FREQUENCIES IS NOT INDEPENDENT OF THE FEATURE IT IS SCORING. *** ⌗ *Which is why the correction went in the direction it did: the old bar was too high, not too low.* &#9670; ⚭⚭⚭ ***AND HERE IS THE PLACEMENT, WHICH IS THE GATE'S AND IS AGAINST THE GATE.*** *The delivering seat reached the third line of `r7033`'s table and refused to choose: "at this bar the projections no longer separate. The fork as posed does not discriminate, and that is the finding rather than a failure to deliver one." **It is right, and the fork was mine.*** &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***THE FORK WAS BUILT ON A DETECTION TEST AND THE CLAUSE ASKS ABOUT AUTHORSHIP, AND THOSE ARE NOT THE SAME QUESTION.*** *A noise null asks whether a quantity is distinguishable from noise. **Every acoustically structured quantity in this decomposition is** --- and the term mix knob moves the balance of two oscillating source terms, so the cost it produces is oscillating BY CONSTRUCTION.* ⇒ *** A TEST EVERY CANDIDATE PASSES CANNOT IDENTIFY A CARRIER, AND `r7033`'s TABLE READ ONE AS IF IT COULD. *** ⌗ ***That is the fifth appearance of one error in this row and the first that is the gate's***: *`cc66.60`'s aggregation error, `cc66.64`'s "a total is not a match", `cc66.65`'s arithmetic-identity gate, `cc66.66`'s leaked null --- **the instrument not matching the question, four times found by the delivering seat against itself and once by it against this seat**.* &#9670; ✔✔ ***AND THE INSTRUMENT THAT DOES MATCH IT WAS BUILT IN THIS ROW ALREADY, BY THE SAME SEAT, ONE REVISION EARLIER.*** *`cc66.65` projected the two spectrum differences like-for-like on the window and got a phase offset of $+0.27$ rad and an amplitude ratio of 0.264, then checked that the ratio predicted the measured amplitude: $0.264\times6.241=1.646$ against 1.893.* ⇒ *** THAT IS AN ACCOUNTING AND NOT A DETECTION, AND AN ACCOUNTING HAS NO THIRD OUTCOME: THE SHARES SUM TO THE WHOLE BY CONSTRUCTION, SO THE ANSWER IS A NUMBER AND THE ONLY QUESTION IS WHERE IT FALLS. *** ⌗ *Ordered at `r7035` on the term mix.* &#9670; ✔ ***70's ROUTED FINDING WAS AMENDED RATHER THAN ACCEPTED OR REJECTED, AND THE AMENDMENT IS BETTER THAN EITHER.*** *The locator's four **peak** anchors sit outside band 1 --- the delivering seat says it could have stopped there and rejected the finding --- **but the instrument is documented as carrying peak AND TROUGH positions, and trough 1 sits at $q=1.360$, inside band 1. The peak-only reading would have been a dodge.** The qualifier: the routine returns a vertex POSITION and never a height.* ⇒ ***It LOCATES inside band 1 and MEASURES no height there***, *so `cc66.62`'s claim survives in substance while the implication it carried --- that the instrument does not reach band 1 at all --- does not.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND 70's SECOND ITEM IS THE ONE THAT CHANGED THE PAPER MOST.*** *Of the three instruments with no noise model: **the contrast statistic as defined never sees the data at all** --- the amplitude it fits to the data cancels exactly against the envelope it divides by, to $10^{-16}$ --- **so it compares two computed spectra and its numerical floor of about six parts in a thousand is the right floor for what it says**. `SRCDEC` cannot be given one for any single term, structurally, since only the sum reaches the sky. The projection-width kernel cannot: its only repository uncertainty is systematic, and the two anchorings read **twelvefold apart** at the top band.* &#9670; ✔ ***BUT THE CONTRAST'S SKY COUNTERPART CAN BE GIVEN A FLOOR, AND IT IS COARSER THAN THE EXCESS IT WOULD MEASURE.*** *From 2,000 draws of the likelihood's covariance: **0.018 whole-range**, against which this arm's $+0.047$ stands at about 2.6 floors --- **but 0.043 to 0.086 PER BAND, where the excess's own per-band values run $+0.022$ to $+0.076$**, with noise biasing every band upward.* ⇒ *** SO BAND BY BAND THE CONTRAST IS A STATEMENT ABOUT THEORIES AND WOULD NOT BE AN OBSERVATION, THE LOWEST BAND STANDING AT UNDER HALF OF ITS OWN BAND'S FLOOR. *** ⌗ *Landed in `P15`, and it is why the rejection this section closes on is read in the likelihood and not in the contrast.* &#9670; ⛔⛔ ***AND TEN PASSAGES OF `P15` RESTED ON A FLOOR THAT DOES NOT EXIST. | **PO-23** | **THE ULTRAVIOLET DEFINITION OF THE MODE SUMS.** &#9670; ***AND THE `r6445` CLAUSE IS RESOLVED r6453 (64) --- STRUCTURALLY, AND IT DOES NOT CLOSE THE ROW.*** *That clause asked whether coupling acts on this sum by deforming the spectrum. **It cannot be established ahead of this row, because it asks what the coupling does to THE SUM and the open item IS the definition of that sum.** ⇒ ***So `60`'s table is exactly the reach of the free-spectrum method, and the remainder is not reachable by refining it*** --- the conditional is the boundary of the technique, not a weakness in the work.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND THIS ROW IS THE LAST OF A NAMED TRIPLE, TWO OF WHICH ARE NOW SAID.*** *`P07`: what remains open is the definition of the interacting tower --- **the spectrum of $\hat\Gamma$, whether it is bounded below, and the ultraviolet definition of the mode sums** --- and then, in its own voice, "**two of those three can now be said**": the operator **is** bounded below, and its spectrum **is** computable branch by branch, $\tfrac14$ under normal ordering and $\tfrac34$ under symmetric with one mode occupied. **Two of the three walls are built.*** &#9670; *And `P10`'s coupling is not what a deformation table deforms: $\hat\Gamma$ acts on the **scale-factor** sector's inverse-square coefficient, a direct integral over the graviton momenta, with the $\mu_n$ untouched --- and the boundary condition is **settled**, thermal regularity supplying it fibre by fibre since $\kappa$ belongs to the background horizon. `P10`'s own note: "**do not report the coupled sector as a residual quantization freedom**".* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE RE-SCOPING THIS INVITES IS ONE THE CORPUS HAS ALREADY MADE AND REVERSED.*** *`P07` frames the item as whether the coupled sector is a consistent well-defined theory, which reads like any interacting theory's problem, and the obvious move is to call the remainder **not CR-specific** as `PO-36` was at `r6407`. **The ledger records that move: "RESTORED `r1291` --- wrongly kill-listed `r1243` as 'not a CR open' and propagated as such this session; a genuine open, not a false-open. Daryl-flagged."*** ⇒ **Not re-made --- and adjudicated rather than deferred to, `r6455`, the restoration being some five thousand revisions old.** *⓵ ***The generic question and this one are not the same object.*** Perturbative quantum gravity's ultraviolet problem is **non-renormalisability --- infinitely many counterterms**. `P10` establishes that **the counterterm basis here is ONE-dimensional**, the admitted background family being one-parameter and the degeneracy conformal flatness. *That is a structural fact about this construction and not the generic situation.* ⓶ *And the object differs upstream of that: **deparametrization SOLVES the constraint**, so there is a true Hamiltonian and the coupling enters $H_{\rm phys}$ as interaction terms rather than as an obstruction, on a **compact** slice with a discrete spectrum of known degeneracies. **A true Hamiltonian with a discrete tower is nearer a quantum-mechanical problem than a covariant field-theoretic one.*** ⓷ ***And the contrast with `PO-36` is sharp rather than a matter of taste***: *there both frameworks take the same $m(r)$, so the measurement **cannot** distinguish them --- which is what made that one not CR-specific. **Here no other framework has this tower, this counterterm basis, or this true Hamiltonian.** The question is about this operator.* ⇒ ***So the remainder is a genuine CR open on its own merits, and room ① does not close.*** \rcpt{P10_the_remainder_is_the_last_of_three_and_is_not_reachable_by_refining_the_method} *`P07` names three parts to "the definition of the interacting tower" and ** &#9670; ***WORKED r6411 (64) --- THE SCOPE CLAUSE SPLITS, AND ONE ROUTE TO THE DISCHARGE IS FORECLOSED AS VACUOUS.*** *"Time-dependence does not move the leading coefficient" is true, and for a stronger reason than an observation on the instantaneous spectrum: for the **free** tower $\omega_n=\mu_n/a$, so $\sum_n d_n\omega_n=(1/a)\sum_n d_n\mu_n$ --- **the scale factor factors out of the whole sum, identically, for every $a$**. It is not that the coefficient is insensitive to $a$; **$a$ never entered the dimensionless coefficient at all**.* ⇒ ***So a time-dependent FREE computation cannot move it and would add nothing. The discharge must be the COUPLED computation and only that***, *because coupling is the one thing that stops the scale factor factoring out.* &#9670; ***AND BOTH OF THIS ROW'S NUMBERS ARE NOW IDENTITIES RATHER THAN AN AGREEMENT.*** *$\zeta(0)=10$ from the terminating expansion ($Z(0)=-5/2$, $Z(-1)=5/2$), and the log coefficient $39/4$ as the exact $1/m$ term of $d(m)\mu(m)=2m^{3}-11m+(39/4)/m+\dots$, with a cancellation-free hard cutoff carrying no zeta function returning $9.74998$. **The row reports "a part in ten thousand"; it is an identity plus a numerical check of it.*** &#9670; ⌗ ***AND ON THIS BACKGROUND THE ONE CONSTANT IS NOT OBSERVABLE, which joins `P10`'s own stronger result.*** *The counterterm basis is one-dimensional because the admitted background family is, and the degeneracy is **conformal flatness** --- no scale factor breaks it. The tower's background $a(T)=\alpha\cosh(T/\alpha)$ is exactly de~Sitter with $R=12/\alpha^{2}$ **constant**, so $\int\!\sqrt g\,R^{2}$ and $\int\!\sqrt g\,R$ are both $\propto V$: **the log's counterterm is degenerate with Einstein--Hilbert and the cosmological term there.** `PO-43` names where a genuinely new one first appears --- Weyl-squared at second order in the shear, which vanishes identically on a conformally flat background.* \rcpt{P10_the_scale_factor_factors_out_of_the_free_tower}two are now settled**: the spectrum of $\hat\Gamma$ is computable branch by branch, and it is bounded below because the full inverse-square coefficient is positive wherever the metric is non-degenerate. **The third is attempted at `r6925`, below, and does not close.*** &#9670; ⛭⚭ ***ATTEMPTED r6925 (66, on node 60's r6920) --- FOR THE FIRST TIME, AND THE ONE ULTRAVIOLET CONSTANT BECOMES OBSERVABLE.*** *The order asked whether $\int\!\sqrt g\,R^{2}$ stays fixed by $\int\!\sqrt g\,R$ and $\int\!\sqrt g$ once $a$ is quantized, and inferred it would not from `P10`'s "part company as soon as $a$ is not the de~Sitter $\cosh$". ⛔ **That inference does not go through, and this seat's reason was wrong where its answer was right**: the degeneracy is equivalent to $R$ being $\sqrt g$-almost-everywhere CONSTANT, which is far weaker, and two non-$\cosh$ things keep it so --- $\Lambda$ with radiation ($R=4\Lambda$ exactly, radiation traceless) and **the free tower's own bare zero-point energy, which is exactly radiation-like** ($E=\mathcal S/a$ with $\mathcal S$ free of $a$, so $\rho\sim a^{-4}$, $p=\rho/3$, trace identically zero). ⇒ *So the naive back-reaction of the quantized tower breaks nothing; both are kept as NULL CONTROLS and both measure rank $1$ to $10^{-43}$, **which is how the numerical floor is measured rather than assumed**.* &#9670; ⛭ ***WHAT BREAKS IT IS THE LOG, WHICH IS TO SAY THE CONSTANT AT ISSUE.*** *$Z(s)$ has a pole at $s=-1$ with residue $\tfrac{39}{4}$, so $E(a)=(1/a)[C_0+r\ln(a\mu)]$ and the $\ln a$ spoils the exact $a^{-4}$ that made the bare sum traceless. **The trace is $\Theta=r/(2\pi^{2}a^{4})$, INDEPENDENT of $\mu$** --- scheme-independent, which is what an anomaly is, and the reason the $C_0$/log split being a convention does not touch the conclusion. Hence $R=4\Lambda+4Gr/(\pi a^{4})$, non-constant iff $r\neq0$, confirmed two independent ways. ⇒ ***RANK 3 at $9.06\times10^{-8}$, thirty-five and a half decades above the measured floor***: the log counterterm is a genuinely new entry, the departure is SIGNED by $I_2I_0-I_1^{2}=I_0^{2}\operatorname{Var}_{\sqrt g}(R)\ge0$, and the gap goes as $r^{2}$, measured constant to $7\times10^{-4}$ over three decades.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE SHAPE OF IT IS THE RESULT, NOT THE RANK.*** *The corpus's no-free-constant claim was saved on this background **by** a degeneracy --- and that degeneracy is switched off by exactly the constant it was hiding. **Were there no log, $R$ would stay constant and the counterterm would stay unobservable; but then there would be no constant to hide.** ⇒ ***The saving mechanism and the thing it saves us from are the same number***, so the claim cannot be rescued this way in the coupled sector. ⌗ *And the CR-specificity guard is pushed the right way rather than tested: the counterterm basis is one-dimensional AT FIXED BACKGROUND and **two-dimensional once the scale factor is quantized**, and the basis's dimension is this row's own CR-specific claim.* &#9670; ⌗ ***ITEM Ⓑ ANSWERS AS A THEOREM RATHER THAN A VALUE.*** *$\zeta(0)=10+B_3'(0)/2$ exactly, only that one coefficient being able to move it, through $\zeta_R$'s pole at argument $1$. **So $\zeta(0)$ is immune to every constant shift, to every multiplicative rescaling --- which contains `r6411`'s "the scale factor factors out" as the single case $L=a^{-2}$ --- and to every shift in even powers of $1/m$.** It moves only on an ODD power of the mode label, in closed form; local operators contribute integer powers of the Laplacian, so **the whole even sector is foreclosed**. ⚠ *A reason to expect $10$ survives coupling and not a proof that it does.*\rcpt{P10_the_degeneracy_needs_r_constant_not_the_cosh_so_the_anomaly_is_what_makes_its_own_constant_observable} &#9670; ⛭⚭ ***AND r6929 (66, on node 60's r6928) SHOWS THE PREMISE WAS NEVER A DEPENDENCY, ON A THIRD BRANCH NEITHER SEAT HAD ENUMERATED.*** *The order costed superselection in $\alpha$ as reversing the finding and non-superselection as leaving it proved. ⇒ ***The two are not alternatives.*** **A superselection rule forbids COHERENCE between sectors and not an ENSEMBLE over them**, and the three integrals are read off the ensemble linearly --- so an ensemble over three distinct $\alpha$ reaches rank $3$ at $1.60\times10^{-4}$, $39$ decades above the floor, ***inside a superselected theory and with no anomaly at all***. And the finding took neither route: two exact rank lemmas separate them, $R$ constant on one history giving rank $1$ for any number of regions and an ensemble over $N$ distinct $\alpha$ giving rank exactly $\min(N,3)$, checked at $N=2$ with the third singular value at $8.2\times10^{-72}$ --- **and the anomaly reaches rank $3$ at $N=1$, where mixing caps the rank at $1$.*** &#9670; ⌗ ***AND THE QUESTION AS PUT HAD NO OBJECT, WHICH IS THE SHARPEST OF THE FIVE CONTROLS.*** *$\alpha=\sqrt{3/\Lambda}$ is a **coefficient** of $H_{\rm phys}$, so $\hat\alpha=\alpha\mathbb{1}$ is central with a ONE-point spectrum against the control's three, and **a central operator with one point decomposes nothing** --- \S`lock` says as much from the other side, $\kappa=1/\alpha$ being common to every fibre. *And $\hat a$, which $R$ actually depends on, is central in nothing*: $[\hat a,H_{\rm phys}]=[\hat a,p_a^{2}]$ exactly and at every order, residual $7.8\times10^{-13}$ against a floor of $7.7\times10^{-12}$ measured on eighteen members of the coupling family. ⇒ *The theory's one genuine superselection label is $\hat\Gamma$ --- central in the **radial** algebra, seven spectral points, **labelling the boundary condition and not the background scale**. ***Centrality is a relation to an algebra***, and that is the two-sided control.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND IT CORRECTS THIS LINE'S OWN LANDED WORK, IN THE DIRECTION OF FEWER WALLS.*** *`r6920` \S E listed as a wall *"if the physical Hilbert space selected a single background, the degeneracy would survive"*. **That clause is false: with a single background the rank is $3$ at $9.06\times10^{-8}$, which is `r6920`'s own headline number, computed at a single $\Lambda$.** ⇒ *The wall list is shorter by one item and not longer, and `P10` carried the chat seat's version of the same clause, which is out.* ⌗ **AND WHAT DOES NEED THE COUPLING IS A DIFFERENT QUESTION FROM THE ONE ASKED:** *whether any physical state makes $\hat R$ sharp --- whether $\operatorname{spec}\hat\Theta$ has a point spectrum. Not computed, stated as the step, and now routed.*\rcpt{P10_the_premise_is_not_load_bearing_because_superselection_forbids_coherence_and_not_the_ensemble} &#9670; ⛭⚭⚭ ***AND r6933 (66, on node 60's r6930) GIVES THE ROW'S SHAPE A THIRD TIME FROM A THIRD DIRECTION: NO STATE RESOLVES THE CURVATURE IT IS READ OFF.*** *The order asked whether $\operatorname{spec}\hat\Theta$ has a point spectrum and expected a measurement; **it answers as a theorem, because the excitation trace vanishes identically AS AN OPERATOR.** With $\omega_n=\mu_n/a$ the excitation energy is $\mathcal S/a$ with $\mathcal S$ free of $a$, so $p=\rho/3$ **in every state and not merely the vacuum**, and the whole anomaly sits in the renormalized zero point as a $c$-number. ⇒ *So $\hat\Theta=(r/2\pi^{2}a^{4})\mathbb{1}$ on the tower and $\hat R=4\Lambda+\kappa\hat a^{-4}$ is a function of $\hat a$ alone --- **which is what makes the question answerable without the coupling**.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND THEN IT IS EXACT RATHER THAN NUMERICAL.*** *$R'(a)=-4\kappa a^{-5}<0$ on the half-line, so $R$ is strictly monotonic hence injective, so every level set above $4\Lambda$ is a single point and below it empty --- and multiplication by $f$ has an eigenvector at $\lambda$ iff $\{f=\lambda\}$ carries positive measure. ⇒ ***So $\hat R$ has NO eigenvectors: purely continuous spectrum, the range $[4\Lambda,\infty)$, with $4\Lambda$ itself not attained.*** ⌗ *The instrument agrees against controls built first --- two refinement exponents at $-1.00$ matching multiplication-by-$x$ and a full decade from the harmonic oscillator's $0.00$, on a measured variance floor of $1.9\times10^{-12}$ --- so the numbers are a control on the theorem rather than the claim, which is the right way round.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE OBSTRUCTION IS THE ANOMALY COEFFICIENT ITSELF, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THIS A ROW AND NOT THREE RESULTS.*** *$[R(\hat a),p_a]=i\hbar R'(\hat a)$ gives $\operatorname{Var}(R)\operatorname{Var}(p_a)\ge(\hbar^{2}/4)\langle R'(\hat a)\rangle^{2}$, saturated to $0.3$ per cent by narrow admissible states, and **the right-hand side vanishes iff $r=0$** --- where $\hat R=4\Lambda\mathbb{1}$ and the variance sits at the floor. ⇒ ***So the same residue that makes the counterterm observable is what forbids any state from resolving the curvature it is read off.*** **`r6920` gave that shape from the trace, `r6928` from the algebra, and this from the spectrum.** ⌗ *And one consistency was not put in by hand: $\langle\hat a^{-4}\rangle$ converges at the origin only above a threshold in the boundary index, and the horizon's own thermal condition clears it on either ordering --- **the boundary condition that closes the deficiency is the same one that makes the curvature's expectation exist**.* &#9670;
